\displaystyle+\frac{A^{2}4^{A+1}}{\mu^{2}}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\|\mathbf{v}_{s}\|^{2}\frac{(s+\gamma)^{2A-2}}{(t+\gamma)^{2A}}

B.3
Proof of Lemma
6

Note that by definition of
E_{t}

\displaystyle\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{t})\|\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{t}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq LD_{t}\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{t}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq L\frac{\sqrt{CR_{T,\delta}}}{(t+\gamma-1)}

Recall that
\Gamma=\tfrac{\mu\sqrt{R_{T,\delta}}}{\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}
i.e.
\sqrt{R_{T,\delta}}=\tfrac{\gamma\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}{\mu}
. Substituting this into the above inequality gives us:

\displaystyle\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{t})\|\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{t}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\kappa\Gamma\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})\sqrt{C}}{t+\gamma-1}

(8)

We recall that
\mathbf{b}_{t}=\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{t})-\mathbb{E}[\mathsf{clip}_{\Gamma}(\mathbf{g}_{t})|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}]=\mathbb{E}[\mathbf{g}_{t}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}]-\mathbb{E}[\mathsf{clip}_{\Gamma}(\mathbf{g}_{t})|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}]
. Since
\mathsf{Cov}[\mathbf{g}_{t}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}]\preceq\Sigma
by Assumption
Bdd.

2
nd

Moment
, we obtain the following bound on
\|\mathbf{b}_{t}\|
by an application of Lemma
4

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{b}_{t}\|\leq\frac{\|\Sigma\|_{2}\sqrt{d_{\mathsf{eff}}}}{\Gamma}+\frac{\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{t})\|\sqrt{\|\Sigma\|_{2}}}{\Gamma}+\frac{\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{t})\|^{3}}{\Gamma^{2}}+\frac{\|\Sigma\|_{2}d_{\mathsf{eff}}\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{t})\|}{\Gamma^{2}}

Since
\tilde{\mathbf{b}}_{t}=\mathbf{b}_{t}\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{t}\right\}
, it follows that

\displaystyle\|\tilde{\mathbf{b}}_{t}\|\leq\underbrace{\frac{\|\Sigma\|_{2}\sqrt{d_{\mathsf{eff}}}}{\Gamma}}_{\textcircled{A}}+\underbrace{\frac{\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{t})\|\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{t}\right\}\sqrt{\|\Sigma\|_{2}}}{\Gamma}}_{\textcircled{B}}+\underbrace{\frac{\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{t})\|^{3}\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{t}\right\}}{\Gamma^{2}}}_{\textcircled{C}}+\underbrace{\frac{\|\Sigma\|_{2}d_{\mathsf{eff}}\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{t})\|\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{t}\right\}}{\Gamma^{2}}}_{\textcircled{D}}

Bounding
\textcircled{A}

By definition of
\Gamma
,

\displaystyle\frac{\|\Sigma\|_{2}\sqrt{d_{\mathsf{eff}}}}{\Gamma}

\displaystyle=\frac{\|\Sigma\|_{2}\sqrt{d_{\mathsf{eff}}}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}{\mu\sqrt{R_{T,\delta}}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(T+\gamma)\|\Sigma\|_{2}\sqrt{d_{\mathsf{eff}}}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}{\mu T\sqrt{R_{T,\delta}}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mu\sqrt{R_{T,\delta}}}{(T+\gamma)}

Hence
\textcircled{A}\leq\frac{\mu\sqrt{R_{T,\delta}}}{T+\gamma}

Bounding
\textcircled{B}

Since
R_{T,\delta}\geq\tfrac{\|\Sigma\|_{2}d_{\mathsf{eff}}(T+\gamma)}{\mu^{2}}\geq\tfrac{\|\Sigma\|_{2}T}{\mu^{2}}
,
\sqrt{\|\Sigma\|_{2}}\leq\tfrac{\mu\sqrt{R_{T,\delta}}}{\sqrt{d(T+\gamma)}}
. Substituting this into equation (
8
),

\displaystyle\frac{\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{t})\|\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{t}\right\}\sqrt{\|\Sigma\|_{2}}}{\Gamma}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\kappa\sqrt{C}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}{t+\gamma-1}\cdot\frac{\mu\sqrt{R_{T,\delta}}}{\sqrt{d(T+\gamma)}}

Hence,
\textcircled{B}\leq\mu\sqrt{R_{T,\delta}}\cdot\frac{\kappa\ln(\nicefrac{{1}}{{\delta}})\sqrt{C}}{(s+\gamma)\sqrt{d(T+\gamma}}

Bounding Ⓒ

From equation (
8
),

\displaystyle\frac{\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{t})\|^{3}}{\Gamma^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\kappa^{3}C^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}\Gamma\ln(\nicefrac{{1}}{{\delta}})^{3}}{(t+\gamma-1)^{3}}

\displaystyle\leq\mu\sqrt{R_{T,\delta}}\cdot\frac{\kappa^{3}C^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}\ln(\nicefrac{{1}}{{\delta}})^{2}}{(t+\gamma-1)^{3}}

Hence,
\textcircled{C}\leq\mu\sqrt{R_{T,\delta}}\cdot\frac{\kappa^{3}C^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}\ln(\nicefrac{{1}}{{\delta}})^{2}}{(t+\gamma-1)^{3}}

Bounding Ⓓ

Recall that,

\displaystyle\|\Sigma\|_{2}d_{\mathsf{eff}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mu^{2}R_{T,\delta}}{T+\gamma}

\displaystyle\frac{\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{t})\|\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{t}\right\}}{\Gamma}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\kappa\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})\sqrt{C}}{(t+\gamma-1)}

\displaystyle\Gamma

\displaystyle=\frac{\mu\sqrt{R_{T,\delta}}}{\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}

It follows that

\displaystyle\textcircled{D}=\frac{\|\Sigma\|_{2}d_{\mathsf{eff}}\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{t})\|\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{t}\right\}}{\Gamma^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\mu\sqrt{R_{T,\delta}}\cdot\frac{\kappa\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{2}\sqrt{C}}{(t+\gamma-1)(T+\gamma)}

Hence,

\displaystyle\|\tilde{\mathbf{b}}_{t}\|\leq\mu\sqrt{R_{T,\delta}}\left(\frac{1}{T+\gamma}+\frac{\kappa\ln(\nicefrac{{1}}{{\delta}})\sqrt{C}}{(t+\gamma-1)\sqrt{d(T+\gamma)}}+\frac{\kappa^{3}C^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}\ln(\nicefrac{{1}}{{\delta}})^{2}}{(t+\gamma-1)^{3}}+\frac{\kappa\sqrt{C}\ln(\nicefrac{{1}}{{\delta}})^{2}}{(t+\gamma-1)(T+\gamma)}\right)

B.4
Proof of Lemma
7

For any
s\in[T]
, we recall that
\mathbf{v}_{s}=\mathbb{E}\left[\mathsf{clip}_{\Gamma}(\mathbf{g}_{s})|\mathcal{F}_{s-1}\right]-\mathsf{clip}_{\Gamma}(\mathbf{g}_{s})
. Since
\mathbb{E}[\mathbf{g}_{s}|\mathcal{F}_{s-1}]=\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{s})
and
\mathsf{Cov}[\mathbf{g}_{s}|\mathcal{F}_{s-1}]\preceq\Sigma
, we obtain the following from Lemma
4

\displaystyle\|\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{v}_{s}\mathbf{v}_{s}^{T}|\mathcal{F}_{s-1}\right]\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\|\mathsf{Cov}\left[\mathsf{clip}_{\Gamma}(\mathbf{g}_{s})|\mathcal{F}_{s-1}\right]\|\leq\|\Sigma\|_{2}+\frac{\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{s})\|^{4}}{\Gamma^{2}}+\frac{\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{s})\|^{2}\mathsf{Tr}(\Sigma)}{\Gamma^{2}}

\displaystyle\mathsf{Tr}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{v}_{s}\mathbf{v}_{s}^{T}|\mathcal{F}_{s-1}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=\mathsf{Tr}\left(\mathsf{Cov}\left[\mathsf{clip}_{\Gamma}(\mathbf{g}_{s})|\mathcal{F}_{s-1}\right]\right)\leq\mathsf{Tr}(\Sigma)

For
s\in[1:T]
define
\mathbb{E}[\tilde{\mathbf{v}}_{s}\tilde{\mathbf{v}}^{T}_{s}|\mathcal{F}_{s-1}]=\tilde{\Sigma}_{s}
. Since
\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{s}\right\}
is
\mathcal{F}_{s-1}
-measurable and
\tilde{\mathbf{v}}_{s}=\mathbf{v}_{s}\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{s}\right\}
, it follows that
\tilde{\Sigma}_{s}=\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{v}_{s}\mathbf{v}_{s}^{T}|\mathcal{F}_{s}\right]\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{s}\right\}
. Hence, we conclude the following from the above inequality

\displaystyle\|\tilde{\Sigma}_{s}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|\Sigma\|_{2}+\frac{\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{s})\|^{4}\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{s}\right\}}{\Gamma^{2}}+\frac{\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{s})\|^{2}\mathsf{Tr}(\Sigma)\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{s}\right\}}{\Gamma^{2}}

\displaystyle\mathsf{Tr}(\tilde{\Sigma}_{s})

\displaystyle\leq\mathsf{Tr}(\Sigma)

(9)

Now, for
s\in[t]
, we define
h_{s}
as follows:

\displaystyle h_{s}=\left\langle\tilde{\mathbf{v}}_{s},\mathbf{d}_{s}\right\rangle\frac{(s+\gamma)^{2A-1}}{(t+\gamma)^{2A-2}}

Note that
\mathbb{E}[h_{s}|\mathcal{F}_{s-1}]=\left\langle\mathbb{E}[\tilde{\mathbf{v}}_{s}|\mathcal{F}_{s-1}],\mathbf{d}_{s}\right\rangle\tfrac{(s+\gamma)^{2A-1}}{(t+\gamma)^{2A-2}}=0
. Furthermore, since
\|\tilde{\mathbf{v}}_{s}\|\leq\|\mathbf{v}_{s}\|\leq 2\Gamma
and
\|\mathbf{d}_{s}\|\leq\tfrac{\sqrt{CR_{T,\delta}}}{s+\gamma-1}

\displaystyle|h_{s}|

\displaystyle\leq 2\Gamma\cdot\frac{\sqrt{CR_{T,\delta}}}{s+\gamma-1}\cdot\frac{(s+\gamma)^{2A-1}}{(t+\gamma)^{2A-2}}

\displaystyle\leq 4\Gamma\sqrt{CR_{T,\delta}}\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{2A-2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4\mu R_{T,\delta}\sqrt{C}}{\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}

(10)

For
s\in[t]
, define
\sigma^{2}_{s}=\mathbb{E}[h^{2}_{s}|\mathcal{F}_{s-1}]
. It follows that,

\displaystyle\sigma^{2}_{s}

\displaystyle=\frac{(s+\gamma)^{4A-2}}{(t+\gamma)^{4A-4}}\mathbf{v}^{T}_{s}\tilde{\Sigma}_{s}\mathbf{v}_{s}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(s+\gamma)^{4A-2}}{(t+\gamma)^{4A-4}}\|\mathbf{v}_{s}\|^{2}\|\tilde{\Sigma}_{s}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq 4CR_{T,\delta}\cdot\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{4A-4}\|\tilde{\Sigma}_{s}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq 4CR_{T,\delta}\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{4A-4}\left(\|\Sigma\|_{2}+\frac{\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{s})\|^{4}}{\Gamma^{2}}+\frac{\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{s})\|^{2}\|\Sigma\|_{2}d_{\mathsf{eff}}}{\Gamma^{2}}\right)

where the last inequality follows from equation (
9
) and the fact that
d_{\mathsf{eff}}=\nicefrac{{\mathsf{Tr}(\Sigma)}}{{\|\Sigma\|_{2}}}
. We now use the above inequality to control
\sum_{s=1}^{t}\sigma^{2}_{s}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})
as follows:

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}\sigma^{2}_{s}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})

\displaystyle\leq 4CR_{T,\delta}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{4A-4}\|\Sigma\|_{2}

\displaystyle+4CR_{T,\delta}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{4A-4}\frac{\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{s})\|^{4}}{\Gamma^{2}}

\displaystyle+4CR_{T,\delta}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{4A-4}\frac{\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{s})\|^{2}\|\Sigma\|_{2}d_{\mathsf{eff}}}{\Gamma^{2}}

(11)

We now control each of the three terms in the above inequality as follows

\displaystyle 4CR_{T,\delta}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{4A-4}\|\Sigma\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq 4CR_{T,\delta}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})\|\Sigma\|_{2}t

\displaystyle\leq 4CtR_{T,\delta}\cdot\frac{\mu^{2}R_{T,\delta}}{(T+\gamma)\sqrt{d_{\mathsf{eff}}}}

\displaystyle\leq 4\mu^{2}CR_{T,\delta}^{2}

Before controlling the remaining two terms, we recall from (
8
) in the proof of Lemma
that

\displaystyle\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{s})\|\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{s}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\kappa\Gamma\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})\sqrt{C}}{s+\gamma-1}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\kappa\Gamma\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})\sqrt{C}}{s+\gamma}

where
\Gamma=\tfrac{\mu\sqrt{R_{T,\delta}}}{\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}
. It follows that

\displaystyle\frac{\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{s})\|^{4}}{\Gamma^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{16\kappa^{4}C^{2}\Gamma^{2}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{4}}{(s+\gamma)^{4}}

\displaystyle=\mu^{2}R_{T,\delta}\cdot\frac{16\kappa^{4}C^{2}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{2}}{(s+\gamma)^{4}}

Thus, we can control the second term in equation (
11
) as follows

\displaystyle 4CR_{T,\delta}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{4A-4}\frac{\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{s})\|^{4}}{\Gamma^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq 64\mu^{2}CR_{T,\delta}^{2}\cdot\kappa^{4}C^{2}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{3}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\frac{(s+\gamma)^{4A-8}}{(t+\gamma)^{4A-4}}

\displaystyle\leq 64\mu^{2}CR_{T,\delta}^{2}\cdot\frac{\kappa^{4}C^{2}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{3}}{(t+\gamma)^{3}}

\displaystyle\leq 64\mu^{2}CR_{T,\delta}^{2}

where the second inequality follows by setting
A\geq 2
and the last inequality follows by setting
\gamma\geq\kappa^{\nicefrac{{4}}{{3}}}C^{\nicefrac{{2}}{{3}}}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})
.

To control the third term in (
11
), we note that by equation (
8
) and the definition of
R_{T,\delta}

\displaystyle\frac{\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{s})\|^{2}\|\Sigma\|_{2}d_{\mathsf{eff}}}{\Gamma^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq 4\mu^{2}R_{T,\delta}\cdot\frac{\kappa^{2}C\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{2}}{(T+\gamma)(s+\gamma)^{2}}

It follows that

\displaystyle 4CR_{T,\delta}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{4A-4}\frac{\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{s})\|^{2}\|\Sigma\|_{2}d_{\mathsf{eff}}}{\Gamma^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq 16\mu^{2}CR_{T,\delta}^{2}\cdot\frac{\kappa^{2}C\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{3}}{T+\gamma}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\frac{(s+\gamma)^{4A-6}}{(t+\gamma)^{4A-4}}

\displaystyle\leq 16\mu^{2}CR_{T,\delta}^{2}\cdot\frac{\kappa^{2}C\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{3}}{(T+\gamma)(t+\gamma)}

\displaystyle\leq 16\mu^{2}CR_{T,\delta}^{2}

where the second inequality follows by setting
A\geq\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}
and the last inequality follows by setting
\gamma\geq\kappa\sqrt{C}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}
. Substituting the above bounds into equation (
11
), we note that

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}\sigma^{2}_{s}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})\leq 84\mu^{2}CR_{T,\delta}

Thus, by Freedman’s inequality (Lemma
3
), we conclude that the following holds with probability at least
1-\nicefrac{{\delta}}{{2}}
uniformly for every
t\in[T]
:

\sum_{s=1}^{t}\frac{(s+\gamma)^{2A-1}}{(t+\gamma)^{2A-2}}\left\langle\tilde{\mathbf{v}}_{s},\mathbf{d}_{s}\right\rangle=\sum_{s=1}^{t}h_{s}\leq 2\sqrt{\sum_{s=1}^{t}\sigma^{2}_{s}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}+8\mu R_{T,\delta}\sqrt{C}\leq 27R_{T,\delta}\sqrt{C}

(12)

To prove the second inequality of this lemma, we define
\mathbf{z}_{s}=\tilde{\mathbf{v}}_{s}\cdot\left(\tfrac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{A-1}
for
s\in[t]
. Note that
\mathbb{E}[\mathbf{z}_{s}|\mathcal{F}_{s-1}]=0
and
\|\mathbf{z}_{s}\|\leq\|\tilde{\mathbf{v}}_{s}\|\leq 2\Gamma
. Define the PSD matrices
\mathbf{G}_{s}=\mathbb{E}[\mathbf{z}_{s}\mathbf{z}^{T}_{s}|\mathcal{F}_{s-1}]=\left(\tfrac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{2A-2}\tilde{\Sigma}_{s}
. Recalling that
\mathsf{Tr}(\tilde{\Sigma}_{s})\leq\mathsf{Tr}(\Sigma)
and the bound obtained on
\|\tilde{\Sigma}_{s}|_{2}
in equation (
9
), we infer the following:

\displaystyle\mathsf{Tr}(\mathbf{G}_{s})

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{2A-2}\mathsf{Tr}(\Sigma)

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{G}_{s}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{2A-2}\|\Sigma\|_{2}+\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{2A-2}\frac{\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{s})\|^{4}\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{s}\right\}}{\Gamma^{2}}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{2A-2}\frac{\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{s})\|^{2}\mathsf{Tr}(\Sigma)\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{s}\right\}}{\Gamma^{2}}

Substituting (
8
) into the bound for
\|\mathbf{G}_{s}\|_{2}
, we obtain the following

\displaystyle\mathsf{Tr}(\mathbf{G}_{s})

\displaystyle\leq q_{s}=\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{2A-2}\mathsf{Tr}(\Sigma)

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{G}_{s}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq p_{s}=\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{2A-2}\|\Sigma\|_{2}+\frac{(s+\gamma)^{2A-6}}{(t+\gamma)^{2A-2}}\cdot 16\kappa^{4}C^{2}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{2}\mu^{2}R_{T,\delta}

\displaystyle+\frac{(s+\gamma)^{2A-4}}{(t+\gamma)^{2A-2}}\cdot 4\kappa^{2}C\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{2}\|\Sigma\|_{2}d_{\mathsf{eff}}

(13)

By Cauchy Schwarz Inequality,

\displaystyle p^{2}_{s}

\displaystyle\leq 3\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{4A-4}\|\Sigma\|^{2}_{2}+3\cdot\frac{(s+\gamma)^{4A-12}}{(t+\gamma)^{4A-4}}\cdot 256\kappa^{8}C^{4}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{4}\mu^{4}R_{T,\delta}^{2}

\displaystyle+3\cdot\frac{(s+\gamma)^{4A-8}}{(t+\gamma)^{4A-4}}\cdot 16\kappa^{4}C^{2}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{4}\|\Sigma\|^{2}_{2}d_{\mathsf{eff}}^{2}

(14)

Since
T\gtrsim\ln(\ln(d))
,
K=\ln(\ln(T))
and
q_{s}\leq\mathsf{Tr}(\Sigma)\ \forall s\in[T]
, our choice of
\Gamma
ensures that the conditions of Corollary
5
are satisfied. Hence, by Corollary
5
, we conclude that the following holds with probability
1-\nicefrac{{\delta}}{{2}}
uniformly for all
t\in[T]

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}\|\mathbf{z}_{s}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 4C_{M}\Gamma^{2}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})+C_{M}\sum_{s=1}^{\mathsf{UP}(t)}q_{s}+\frac{C_{M}t}{4\Gamma^{2}}\sum_{s=1}^{\mathsf{UP}(t)}p^{2}_{s}

Simplifying the above using equations (
13
), (
14
) and the definition of
\Gamma
, we obtain the following inequality which holds with probability at least
1-\nicefrac{{\delta}}{{2}}
uniformly for every
t\in[T]
:

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}\|\mathbf{z}_{s}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 4C_{M}\mu^{2}R_{T,\delta}+C_{M}\sum_{s=1}^{\mathsf{UP}(t)}\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{2A-2}\mathsf{Tr}(\Sigma)+\frac{3C_{M}}{4}\sum_{s=1}^{\mathsf{UP}(t)}\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{4A-4}\frac{t\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{2}\|\Sigma\|^{2}}{\mu^{2}R_{T,\delta}}

\displaystyle+\frac{3C_{M}}{4}\sum_{s=1}^{\mathsf{UP}(t)}\frac{(s+\gamma)^{4A-12}}{(t+\gamma)^{4A-4}}\cdot 256t\kappa^{8}C^{4}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{6}\mu^{2}R_{T,\delta}

\displaystyle+\frac{3C_{M}}{4}\sum_{s=1}^{\mathsf{UP}(t)}\frac{(s+\gamma)^{4A-8}}{(t+\gamma)^{4A-4}}\frac{16t\kappa^{4}C^{2}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{6}\mathsf{Tr}(\Sigma)^{2}}{\mu^{2}R_{T,\delta}}

(15)

We now simplify each term in the above inequality by using the fact that
\mathsf{UP}(t)\leq\min\{T,2t\}
. To this end, the second term is simplified as follows by using the fact that
A\geq 1

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{\mathsf{UP}(t)}\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{4A-4}\mathsf{Tr}(\Sigma)

\displaystyle\leq\mathsf{UP}(t)\mathsf{Tr}(\Sigma)\leq\mu^{2}R_{T,\delta}

We now control the third term as follows using the definition of
R_{T,\delta}
and the fact that
A\geq 1
:

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{\mathsf{UP}(t)}\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{4A-4}\frac{t\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{2}\|\Sigma\|^{2}}{\mu^{2}R_{T,\delta}}

\displaystyle\leq\mu^{2}R_{T,\delta}\cdot\frac{t\mathsf{UP}(t)}{d(T+\gamma)^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\mu^{2}R_{T,\delta}

To control the fourth term, we use the fact that
A\geq 3
and note that for
s\leq 2t
,
(s+\gamma)\leq 2(t+\gamma)

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{\mathsf{UP}(t)}\frac{(s+\gamma)^{4A-12}}{(t+\gamma)^{4A-4}}\cdot 256t\kappa^{8}C^{4}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{6}\mu^{2}R_{T,\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\mu^{2}R_{T,\delta}2^{8}\kappa^{8}C^{4}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{6}\sum_{s=1}^{2t}\frac{(s+\gamma)^{4A-12}}{(t+\gamma)^{4A-4}}

\displaystyle\leq\mu^{2}R_{T,\delta}\cdot\frac{t^{2}2^{4A-3}\kappa^{8}C^{4}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{6}}{(t+\gamma)^{8}}

\displaystyle\leq\mu^{2}R_{T,\delta}\cdot\frac{2^{4A-3}\kappa^{8}C^{4}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{6}}{(t+\gamma)^{6}}

\displaystyle\leq\mu^{2}R_{T,\delta}2^{4A-15}

where the last inequality follows by setting
\gamma\geq 4\kappa^{\nicefrac{{4}}{{3}}}C^{\nicefrac{{4}}{{3}}}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})

We control the last term by a similar argument

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{\mathsf{UP}(t)}\frac{(s+\gamma)^{4A-8}}{(t+\gamma)^{4A-4}}\frac{16t\kappa^{4}C^{2}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{6}\mathsf{Tr}(\Sigma)^{2}}{\mu^{2}R_{T,\delta}}

\displaystyle\leq\mu^{2}R_{T,\delta}\cdot\frac{t}{(T+\gamma)^{2}}\cdot 2^{4}\kappa^{4}C^{2}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{6}\sum_{s=1}^{2t}\frac{(s+\gamma)^{4A-8}}{(t+\gamma)^{4A-4}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{t^{2}}{(T+\gamma)^{2}(t+\gamma)^{4}}\cdot 2^{4A-3}\kappa^{4}C^{2}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{6}

\displaystyle\leq 2^{4A-11}\mu^{2}R_{T,\delta}

where the last inequality follows by setting
\gamma\geq 4\kappa\sqrt{C}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}
. Substituting the obtained bounds into equation (
15
), we conclude that the following holds with probability at least
1-\nicefrac{{\delta}}{{2}}
uniformly for every
t\in[T]
:

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{2A-2}\|\tilde{\mathbf{v}}_{s}\|^{2}=\sum_{s=1}^{t}\|\mathbf{z}_{s}\|^{2}\leq C_{M}\mu^{2}R_{T,\delta}\left(6+3\cdot 2^{4A-13}+3\cdot 2^{4A-17}\right)

The proof is completed via a union bound.

Appendix C
Analysis for Smooth Strongly Convex Functions Under Quadratic Growth Noise Model

Following a convention similar to that of Section
B
, let
K=4\max\{8,C_{M},\ln(T)\}
. For
t\geq 1
, define the filtration
\mathcal{F}_{t}=\sigma\left(\mathbf{x}_{1},\mathbf{g}_{s}|1\leq s\leq t\right)
and
\mathcal{F}_{0}=\sigma(\mathbf{x}_{1})
. Furthermore, let
\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{t})=\mathsf{clip}_{\Gamma}(\mathbf{g}_{t})+\mathbf{b}_{t}+\mathbf{v}_{t}
where

\mathbf{b}_{t}=\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{t})-\mathbb{E}[\mathsf{clip}_{\Gamma}(\mathbf{g}_{t})|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}]
and
\mathbf{v}_{t}=\mathbb{E}[\mathsf{clip}_{\Gamma}(\mathbf{g}_{t})|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}]-\mathsf{clip}_{\Gamma}(\mathbf{g}_{t})
. As beforem, we note that
\mathbb{E}[\mathbf{v}_{t}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}]=0
and
\|\mathbf{v}_{t}\|\leq 2\Gamma
. Hence
\mathbf{v}_{t}
is an
\mathcal{F}
adapted almost surely bounded martingale difference sequence. Now, let
D_{t}=\|\mathbf{x}_{t}-\mathbf{x}^{*}\|
where
\mathbf{x}^{*}
is the unique minimizer of
F
(guaranteed by strong convexity). We also define
\Sigma_{t}=\Sigma(\mathbf{x}_{t})
and note that
\|\Sigma_{t}\|\leq\alpha D^{2}_{t}+\beta
and
\mathsf{Tr}(\Sigma_{t})\leq d_{\mathsf{eff}}\left(\alpha D^{2}_{t}+\beta\right)
. Furthermore
\Sigma_{t}
is
\mathcal{F}_{t-1}
measurable. Let
\eta_{t}=\tfrac{A}{t+\gamma}
where
A\geq 1
is a numerical constant and
\gamma\geq A\kappa+A-1
is a constant depending on
\kappa,d
and
\ln(\nicefrac{{1}}{{\delta}})
which we shall specify later. Note that our choice of
\gamma
ensures that
\eta_{t}\leq\tfrac{1}{L+\mu}
for
t\in[1:T]
An application of Lemma
5
shows that
D_{t}
satisfies the following for every
t\in[1:T]

\displaystyle D^{2}_{t+1}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{\gamma+1}{t+\gamma}\right)^{2A}D^{2}_{1}+\frac{A2^{2A+1}}{\mu}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\frac{(s+\gamma-1)^{2A-1}}{(t+\gamma)^{2A}}\left\langle\mathbf{b}_{s},\mathbf{x}_{s}-\mathbf{x}^{*}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\frac{A^{2}4^{A+1}}{\mu^{2}}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\|\mathbf{b}_{s}\|^{2}\frac{(s+\gamma)^{2A-2}}{(t+\gamma)^{2A}}+\frac{A2^{2A+1}}{\mu}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\frac{(s+\gamma)^{2A-1}}{(t+\gamma)^{2A}}\left\langle\mathbf{v}_{s},\mathbf{x}_{s}-\mathbf{x}^{*}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\frac{A^{2}4^{A+1}}{\mu^{2}}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\|\mathbf{v}_{s}\|^{2}\frac{(s+\gamma)^{2A-2}}{(t+\gamma)^{2A}}

We now define
R_{T,\delta}
as follows:

\displaystyle R_{T,\delta}=(\gamma+1)^{2}D^{2}_{1}+\frac{(T+\gamma)\beta}{\mu^{2}}\left(d_{\mathsf{eff}}+\sqrt{d_{\mathsf{eff}}}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})\right)

It is easy to see that
\Gamma=\tfrac{\mu\sqrt{R_{T,\delta}}}{\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}
. In our proof of Theorem
1
, we shall establish that the following holds with probability at least
1-\delta
:

\displaystyle D^{2}_{t}\leq\frac{CR_{T,\delta}}{(t+\gamma-1)^{2}}\ \forall\ t\in[1:T+1]

where
C>0
is an absolute numerical constant to be chosen later. To this end, we define the event
E_{t}
and the
\mathcal{F}_{t}
measurable random variables
\mathbf{d}_{t},\tilde{\mathbf{b}}_{t},\tilde{\mathbf{v}}_{t}
as follows for
t\in[1:T+1]
:

\displaystyle E_{t}

\displaystyle=\left\{D^{2}_{t}\leq\frac{CR_{T,\delta}}{(t+\gamma-1)^{2}}\right\}

\displaystyle\mathbf{d}_{t}

\displaystyle=(\mathbf{x}_{t}-\mathbf{x}^{*})\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{t}\right\}

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathbf{b}}_{t}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{b}_{t}\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{t}\right\}

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathbf{v}}_{t}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{v}_{t}\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{t}\right\}

We use the following Lemma to control the bias vector
\tilde{\mathbf{b}}_{t}

Lemma 8

(Bias Control)
.

The following holds almost surely for every
t\in[1:T]
:

\displaystyle\|\tilde{\mathbf{b}}_{t}\|\leq\mu\sqrt{R_{T,\delta}}\sum_{j=1}^{7}B_{j}

where
B_{1},\dots,B_{7}
are defined as follows:

\displaystyle B_{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T+\gamma},

\displaystyle B_{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{4\alpha C\sqrt{d}\ln(\nicefrac{{\ln(T)}}{{\delta}})}{\mu^{2}(s+\gamma)^{2}},

\displaystyle B_{3}

\displaystyle=\frac{2\kappa\sqrt{C}\ln(\nicefrac{{\ln(T)}}{{\delta}})}{(s+\gamma)\sqrt{d(T+\gamma)}},

\displaystyle B_{4}

\displaystyle=\frac{4\kappa C\ln(\nicefrac{{\ln(T)}}{{\delta}})\sqrt{\alpha}}{\mu(s+\gamma)^{2}},

\displaystyle B_{5}

\displaystyle=\frac{8\kappa^{3}C^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}\ln(\nicefrac{{\ln(T)}}{{\delta}})^{2}}{(s+\gamma)^{3}},

\displaystyle B_{6}

\displaystyle=\frac{2\kappa\sqrt{C}\ln(\nicefrac{{\ln(T)}}{{\delta}})^{2}}{(s+\gamma)(T+\gamma)},

\displaystyle B_{7}

\displaystyle=\frac{8\alpha\kappa d\ln(\nicefrac{{\ln(T)}}{{\delta}})^{2}C^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}}{\mu^{2}(s+\gamma)^{3}}

We use the following lemma to control the variance vector
\tilde{\mathbf{v}}_{t}
. The proof of this lemma, which uses Freedman’s inequality and the PAC Bayesian martingale concentration inequality of Corollary
6
.

Lemma 9

(Variance Control)
.

The following holds with probability at least
1-\delta
uniformly for every
t\in[T]
for
A\geq 3
and
\gamma\geq 4C\max\{\frac{\alpha d_{\mathsf{eff}}}{\mu^{2}},\frac{\alpha\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}{\mu^{2}},\kappa^{\nicefrac{{4}}{{3}}}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}}),\kappa\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}},\frac{\kappa^{\nicefrac{{2}}{{3}}}d_{\mathsf{eff}}^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{3}}}\alpha^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{3}}}}{\mu^{\nicefrac{{2}}{{3}}}}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})\}

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}\frac{(s+\gamma)^{2A-1}}{(t+\gamma)^{2A-2}}\left\langle\tilde{\mathbf{v}}_{s},\mathbf{d}_{s}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\lesssim 34\cdot\mu R_{T,\delta}\sqrt{C}

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{2A-2}\|\tilde{\mathbf{v}}_{s}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim C_{M}\left(2^{4A-3}\frac{25}{4}+5\cdot 2^{4A-11}+5\cdot 2^{4A-16}+5\cdot 2^{4A-13}\right)\mu^{2}R_{T,\delta}

where
C_{M}
is the absolute numerical constant defined in Corollary
5
.

Equipped with this bound on the bias and the variance, we now present the complete proof as follows:

C.1
Proof of Theorem
2

Proof.

Let
A\geq 3
,
\gamma\geq 4C\max\{\frac{\alpha d_{\mathsf{eff}}}{\mu^{2}},\frac{\alpha\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}{\mu^{2}},\kappa^{\nicefrac{{4}}{{3}}}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}}),\kappa\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}},\frac{\kappa^{\nicefrac{{2}}{{3}}}d_{\mathsf{eff}}^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{3}}}\alpha^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{3}}}}{\mu^{\nicefrac{{2}}{{3}}}}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})\}
. Now, let
E
denote the following event

\displaystyle E=\{

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}\frac{(s+\gamma)^{2A-1}}{(t+\gamma)^{2A-2}}\left\langle\tilde{\mathbf{v}}_{s},\mathbf{d}_{s}\right\rangle\leq 34\cdot\mu R_{T,\delta}\sqrt{C}\ \forall\ t\in[T]

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{2A-2}\|\tilde{\mathbf{v}}_{s}\|^{2}\leq 53\cdot C_{M}\mu^{2}R_{T,\delta}\ \forall t\in[T]\}

Note that by Lemma
9
,
\mathbb{P}(E)\geq 1-\delta
. We now claim that
\mathbb{P}\left(\bigcap_{t=1}^{T+1}E_{t}|E\right)=1
, i.e., conditioned on the event
E
, the following holds almost surely for every
t\in[1:T+1]

\displaystyle D^{2}_{t}\leq\frac{CR_{T,\delta}}{(t+\gamma-1)^{2}}\ \forall\ t\in[1:T+1]

We prove the above claim by induction. Note that the claim is trivially true for
t=1
as
R_{T,\delta}\geq(\gamma+1)^{2}D^{2}_{1}
. Now, consider any
t\in[1:T]
and suppose the claim holds for some
1\leq s\leq t
.

Recall that by Lemma
5

\displaystyle(t+\gamma)^{2}D^{2}_{t+1}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(\gamma+1)^{2A}}{(t+\gamma)^{2A-2}}D^{2}_{1}+\frac{A2^{2A+1}}{\mu}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\frac{(s+\gamma-1)^{2A-1}}{(t+\gamma)^{2A-2}}\left\langle\mathbf{b}_{s},\mathbf{x}_{s}-\mathbf{x}^{*}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\frac{A^{2}4^{A+1}}{\mu^{2}}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\|\mathbf{b}_{s}\|^{2}\frac{(s+\gamma)^{2A-2}}{(t+\gamma)^{2A-2}}+\frac{A2^{2A+1}}{\mu}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\frac{(s+\gamma)^{2A-1}}{(t+\gamma)^{2A-2}}\left\langle\mathbf{v}_{s},\mathbf{x}_{s}-\mathbf{x}^{*}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\frac{A^{2}4^{A+1}}{\mu^{2}}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\|\mathbf{v}_{s}\|^{2}\frac{(s+\gamma)^{2A-2}}{(t+\gamma)^{2A-2}}

Under the induction hypothesis,
\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{s}\right\}=1\ \forall s\in[t]
. Hence,
Under the induction hypothesis,
\mathbbm{1}\left\{D^{2}_{s}\leq\tfrac{CR_{T,\delta}}{(s+\gamma-1)(s+\gamma-2)}\right\}=1
and thus,
\mathbf{d}_{s}=\mathbf{x}_{s}-\mathbf{x}^{*},\mathbf{b}_{s}=\tilde{\mathbf{b}}_{s},\mathbf{v}_{s}=\tilde{\mathbf{v}}_{s}\ \forall\ 1\leq s\leq t
. Substituting this transformation into the above inequality, we obtain the following:

\displaystyle(t+\gamma)^{2}D^{2}_{t+1}

\displaystyle\leq\underbrace{\frac{(\gamma+1)^{2A}}{(t+\gamma)^{2A-2}}D^{2}_{1}}_{\textcircled{1}}+\underbrace{\frac{A2^{2A+1}}{\mu}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\frac{(s+\gamma)^{2A-1}}{(t+\gamma)^{2A-2}}\left\langle\tilde{\mathbf{v}}_{s},\mathbf{d}_{s}\right\rangle}_{\textcircled{2}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\frac{A^{2}4^{A+1}}{\mu^{2}}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\|\tilde{\mathbf{v}}_{s}\|^{2}\frac{(s+\gamma)^{2A-2}}{(t+\gamma)^{2A-2}}}_{\textcircled{3}}+\underbrace{\sum_{s=1}^{t}\frac{(s+\gamma)^{2A-1}}{(t+\gamma)^{2A-2}}\left\langle\tilde{\mathbf{b}}_{s},\mathbf{d}_{s}\right\rangle}_{\textcircled{4}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\frac{A^{2}4^{A+1}}{\mu^{2}}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\|\tilde{\mathbf{b}}_{s}\|^{2}\frac{(s+\gamma)^{2A-2}}{(t+\gamma)^{2A-2}}}_{\textcircled{5}}

(16)

We now bound each of the terms in the RHS as follows.

Bounding
\textcircled{1}

Since
A\geq 1
and
t\geq 1
,

\displaystyle\textcircled{1}=\frac{(\gamma+1)^{2A}}{(t+\gamma)^{2A-2}}D^{2}_{1}\leq(\gamma+1)^{2}D^{2}_{1}\leq R_{T,\delta}

Bounding
\textcircled{2}

Since
\gamma
and
A
satisfy the conditions of Lemma
7
and we have conditioned on the event
E
, it follows that:

\displaystyle\frac{A2^{2A+1}}{\mu}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\frac{(s+\gamma)^{2A-1}}{(t+\gamma)^{2A-2}}\left\langle\tilde{\mathbf{v}}_{s},\mathbf{d}_{s}\right\rangle\leq 17A4^{A+1}R_{T,\delta}\sqrt{C}

Bounding
\textcircled{3}

Since
\gamma
and
A
satisfy the conditions of Lemma
7
and we have conditioned on the event
E
, it follows that:

\displaystyle\frac{A^{2}4^{A+1}}{\mu^{2}}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{2A-2}\|\tilde{\mathbf{v}}_{s}\|^{2}\leq A^{2}2^{2A+2}C_{M}\left(2^{4A-3}\frac{25}{4}+5\cdot 2^{4A-11}+5\cdot 2^{4A-16}+5\cdot 2^{4A-13}\right)R_{T,\delta}

Bounding
\textcircled{4}

Since
\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{s}\right\}=1

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{d}_{s}\|\leq\frac{\sqrt{CR_{T,\delta}}}{s+\gamma-1}\leq\frac{2\sqrt{CR_{T,\delta}}}{s+\gamma}

Hence, by Lemma
8

\displaystyle\frac{A2^{2A+1}}{\mu}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left\langle\tilde{\mathbf{b}}_{s},\mathbf{d}_{s}\right\rangle\frac{(s+\gamma)^{2A-1}}{(t+\gamma)^{2A-2}}\leq A2^{2A+2}R_{T,\delta}\sqrt{C}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{2A-2}\sum_{j=1}^{7}B_{j}

We now control the first term

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{2A-2}B_{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T+\gamma}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{2A-2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{t}{T+\gamma}\leq 1

where the first inequality follows from the fact that
A\geq 1
and
s\leq t
.

We now control the second term

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{2A-2}B_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4\alpha C\sqrt{d}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}{\mu^{2}}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\frac{(s+\gamma)^{2A-4}}{(t+\gamma)^{2A-2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4\alpha C\sqrt{d}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}{\mu^{2}(t+\gamma)}\leq 1

where the first inequality follows from the fact that
A\geq 2
and
s\leq t
and the second inequality follows by setting
\gamma\geq\frac{4\alpha C\sqrt{d}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}{\mu^{2}}
.

We now bound the third term as follows:

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{2A-2}B_{3}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\kappa\sqrt{C}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}{\sqrt{d(T+\gamma)}}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\frac{(s+\gamma)^{2A-3}}{(t+\gamma)^{2A-2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\kappa\sqrt{C}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}{\sqrt{d(T+\gamma)}}\leq 1

where we use the fact that
A\geq 2
and set
\gamma\geq\frac{4\kappa^{2}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{2}}{d}
.

We now bound the fourth term as follows:

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{2A-2}B_{4}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4\kappa C\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})\sqrt{\alpha}}{\mu}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\frac{(s+\gamma)^{2A-4}}{(t+\gamma)^{2A-2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4\kappa C\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})\sqrt{\alpha}}{\mu(t+\gamma)}\leq 1

where
A\geq 2
and
\gamma\geq\frac{4\kappa C\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})\sqrt{\alpha}}{\mu}

We now bound the fifth term as follows

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{2}B_{5}

\displaystyle\leq 8\kappa^{3}C^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{2}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\frac{(s+\gamma)^{2A-5}}{(t+\gamma)^{[}2A-2]}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{8\kappa^{3}C^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{2}}{(t+\gamma)^{2}}\leq 1

where
A\geq 3
and
\gamma\geq 4\kappa^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}C^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{4}}}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})
.

We now bound the sixth term as follows

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{2A-2}B_{6}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\kappa\sqrt{C}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{2}}{T+\gamma}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\frac{(s+\gamma)^{2A-3}}{(t+\gamma)^{2A-2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\kappa\sqrt{C}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{2}}{T+\gamma}\leq 1

where
A\geq 3
and
\gamma\geq 2\kappa\sqrt{C}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{2}

Finally, we control the seventh term as follows

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{2A-2}B_{7}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{8\alpha\kappa d\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{2}C^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}}{\mu^{2}}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\frac{(s+\gamma)^{2A-5}}{(t+\gamma)^{2A-2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{8\alpha\kappa d\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{2}C^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}}{\mu^{2}(t+\gamma)^{2}}\leq 1

where
A\geq 3
and
\gamma\geq\frac{4\sqrt{\alpha\kappa d}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})C^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{4}}}}{\mu}
.
Putting it all together, it follows that

\displaystyle\textcircled{4}\leq 7A4^{A+1}R_{T,\delta}\sqrt{C}

by setting
\gamma
as follows

\displaystyle\gamma\geq 4C\max\left\{\frac{\alpha\sqrt{d}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}{\mu^{2}},\frac{\kappa^{2}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{2}}{d},\frac{\kappa\sqrt{\alpha}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}{\mu},\kappa^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}}),\kappa\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{2},\frac{\sqrt{\kappa\alpha d}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}{\mu}\right\}

Bounding ⑤

By Lemma
8
and Jensen’s inequality

\displaystyle\|\tilde{\mathbf{b}}_{s}\|^{2}\leq 7\mu^{2}R_{T,\delta}\sum_{j=1}^{7}B^{2}_{j}

It follows that

\displaystyle\frac{A^{2}2^{2A+2}}{\mu^{2}}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\|\tilde{\mathbf{b}}_{s}\|^{2}\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{2A-2}

\displaystyle\leq 7A^{2}2^{2A+2}R_{T,\delta}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{2A-2}\sum_{j=1}^{7}B^{2}_{j}

The first term is controlled as follows using the fact that
A\geq 1

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{2A-2}B^{2}_{1}

\displaystyle=\sum_{s=1}^{t}\frac{1}{(T+\gamma)^{2}}\leq 1

The second term is controlled as

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{2A-2}B^{2}_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{16\alpha^{2}C^{2}d\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{2}}{\mu^{4}}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\frac{(s+\gamma)^{2A-6}}{(t+\gamma)^{2A-2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{16\alpha^{2}C^{2}d\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{2}}{\mu^{4}(t+\gamma)^{3}}\leq 1

where
A\geq 3
and
\gamma\geq\frac{2^{\nicefrac{{4}}{{3}}}\alpha^{\nicefrac{{2}}{{3}}}C^{\nicefrac{{2}}{{3}}}d^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{3}}}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{\nicefrac{{2}}{{3}}}}{\mu^{\nicefrac{{4}}{{3}}}}
.

The third term is controlled as

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{2A-2}B^{2}_{3}

\displaystyle=\frac{4\kappa^{2}C\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{2}}{d(T+\gamma)}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\frac{(s+\gamma)^{2A-4}}{(t+\gamma)^{2A-2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4\kappa^{2}C\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{2}}{d(t+\gamma)(T+\gamma)}\leq 1

where the last inequality follows because
\gamma\geq\kappa\sqrt{\tfrac{C}{d}}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})

The fourth term is controlled as

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{2A-2}B^{2}_{4}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{16\kappa^{2}C^{2}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{2}\alpha}{\mu^{2}}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\frac{(s+\gamma)^{2A-6}}{(t+\gamma)^{2A-2}}

where
A\geq 3
and
\gamma\geq\frac{2^{\nicefrac{{4}}{{3}}}\kappa^{\nicefrac{{2}}{{3}}}C^{\nicefrac{{2}}{{3}}}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{\nicefrac{{2}}{{3}}}\alpha^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{3}}}}{\mu^{\nicefrac{{2}}{{3}}}}

For controlling the fifth term, we set
A\geq 4
to obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{2A-2}B^{2}_{5}

\displaystyle=\kappa^{6}C^{3}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{4}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\frac{(s+\gamma)^{2A-8}}{(t+\gamma)^{2A-2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\kappa^{6}C^{3}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{4}}{(\gamma+1)^{5}}\leq 1

where the last inequality uses the fact that
\gamma\geq\kappa^{\nicefrac{{6}}{{5}}}C^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{5}}}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{\nicefrac{{4}}{{5}}}

To control the sixth term, we use the fact that
A\geq 2
to obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{2A-2}B^{2}_{6}

\displaystyle=\frac{\kappa^{2}C\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{4}}{(T+\gamma)^{2}}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\frac{(s+\gamma)^{2A-4}}{(t+\gamma)^{2A-2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\kappa^{2}C\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{4}}{(\gamma+1)^{3}}\leq 1

where the last inequality uses the fact that
\gamma\geq\kappa^{\nicefrac{{2}}{{3}}}C^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{3}}}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{\nicefrac{{4}}{{3}}}

To control the seventh term, we set
A\geq 4
to obtain the following:

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{2A-2}B^{2}_{6}

\displaystyle=\frac{64\alpha^{2}\kappa^{2}d\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{4}C^{3}}{\mu^{4}}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\frac{(s+\gamma)^{2A-8}}{(t+\gamma)^{2A-2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{64\alpha^{2}\kappa^{2}d\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{4}C^{3}}{\mu^{4}(t+\gamma)^{5}}\leq 1

where
\gamma\geq\frac{2^{\nicefrac{{6}}{{5}}}\alpha^{\nicefrac{{2}}{{5}}}\kappa^{\nicefrac{{2}}{{5}}}d^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{5}}}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{\nicefrac{{4}}{{5}}}C^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{5}}}}{\mu^{\nicefrac{{4}}{{5}}}}

From the obtained bounds, we conclude that
\textcircled{5}\leq 49A^{2}4^{A+1}R_{T,\delta}
.

Now, we set
A=4
and
\gamma
as follows:

\displaystyle\gamma

\displaystyle=\max\left\{\frac{\alpha d}{\mu^{2}},\frac{\alpha\sqrt{d}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}{\mu^{2}},\frac{\kappa\sqrt{\alpha}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}{\mu^{2}},\frac{\sqrt{\kappa\alpha d}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}{\mu},\frac{\kappa^{\nicefrac{{2}}{{3}}}d^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{3}}}\alpha^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{3}}}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}{\mu^{\nicefrac{{2}}{{3}}}},\kappa^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}}),\kappa\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{2},\frac{\kappa^{2}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}{d}\right\}

Under this setting of
A
and
\gamma
, we obtain the following

\displaystyle(t+\gamma)^{2}D^{2}_{t+1}

\displaystyle\leq\textcircled{1}+\textcircled{2}+\textcircled{3}+\textcircled{4}+\textcircled{5}

\displaystyle\leq R_{T,\delta}[1+A^{2}2^{2A+2}C_{M}\left(2^{4A-3}\frac{25}{4}+5\cdot 2^{4A-11}+5\cdot 2^{4A-16}+5\cdot 2^{4A-13}\right)

\displaystyle+49A^{2}4^{A+1}+24A4^{A+1}\sqrt{C}]

\displaystyle\leq R_{T,\delta}\left(802817+6946816C_{M}+98304\sqrt{C}\right)

\displaystyle\leq CR_{T,\delta}

where the second inequality holds due to our choice of
A
and
\gamma
and the last inequality is obtained by setting
C=\left(\sqrt{802817+6946816C_{M}}+98304\right)^{2}
. It follows that

\displaystyle D^{2}_{t+1}\leq\frac{CR_{T,\delta}}{(t+\gamma)^{2}}

Thus, we have proved by induction that conditioned on
E
,
D^{2}_{t}\leq\tfrac{CR_{T,\delta}}{(t+\gamma-1)^{2}}
for every
t\in[T+1]
. In particular, the following holds with probability at least
1-\delta
:

\displaystyle D^{2}_{T+1}\leq C\left(\frac{\gamma+1}{T+\gamma}\right)^{2}D^{2}_{1}+\frac{C\beta\left(d_{\mathsf{eff}}+\sqrt{d_{\mathsf{eff}}}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})\right)}{\mu^{2}(T+\gamma)}

∎

C.2
Proof of Lemma
8

Following the same steps as in that of the proof of Lemma
6
, we use Lemma
4
and the fact that
\mathsf{Cov}[\mathbf{g}_{t}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}]=\Sigma_{t}
to obtain:

\displaystyle\|\tilde{\mathbf{b}}_{s}\|\leq\underbrace{\frac{\|\Sigma_{s}\|\sqrt{d_{\mathsf{eff}}}\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{s}\right\}}{\Gamma}}_{\textcircled{A}}+\underbrace{\frac{\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{s})\|\sqrt{\|\Sigma_{s}\|}\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{s}\right\}}{\Gamma}}_{\textcircled{B}}+\underbrace{\frac{\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{s})\|^{3}\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{s}\right\}}{\Gamma^{2}}}_{\textcircled{C}}+\underbrace{\frac{\|\Sigma_{s}\|d_{\mathsf{eff}}\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{s})\|\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{s}\right\}}{\Gamma^{2}}}_{\textcircled{D}}

Bounding Ⓐ

Note that by Assumption
QG

2
nd

Moment

\displaystyle\|\Sigma_{s}\|_{2}\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{s}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq(\beta+\alpha D^{2}_{s})\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{s}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\beta+\frac{4\alpha CR_{T,\delta}}{(s+\gamma)^{2}}

It follows that

\displaystyle\frac{\|\Sigma_{s}\|_{2}\sqrt{d}\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{s}\right\}}{\Gamma}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\beta\sqrt{d_{\mathsf{eff}}}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}{\mu\sqrt{R_{T,\delta}}}+\frac{4\alpha C\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})\sqrt{R_{T,\delta}d_{\mathsf{eff}}}}{\mu(s+\gamma)^{2}}

Since
\beta\sqrt{d_{\mathsf{eff}}}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})\leq\tfrac{\mu^{2}R_{T,\delta}}{T+\gamma}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\textcircled{A}=\frac{\|\Sigma\|_{s}\sqrt{d}\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{s}\right\}}{\Gamma}

\displaystyle\leq\mu\sqrt{R_{T,\delta}}\left(\frac{1}{T+\gamma}+\frac{4\alpha C\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})\sqrt{R_{T,\delta}d_{\mathsf{eff}}}}{\mu^{2}(s+\gamma)^{2}}\right)

Bounding Ⓑ

Note that by equation (
8
),

\displaystyle\frac{\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{s})\|\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{s}\right\}}{\Gamma}\leq\frac{2\kappa\sqrt{C}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}{s+\gamma}

Furthermore, by Assumption
QG

2
nd

Moment
and the definition of
E_{s}

\displaystyle\sqrt{\|\Sigma_{s}\|_{2}}\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{s}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\beta}+\frac{2\sqrt{\alpha CR_{T,\delta}}}{s+\gamma}

Recalling that
\beta\leq\tfrac{\mu^{2}R_{T,\delta}}{d_{\mathsf{eff}}(T+\gamma)}
,

\displaystyle\frac{\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{s})\|\sqrt{\|\Sigma_{s}\|_{2}}\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{s}\right\}}{\Gamma}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\kappa\sqrt{C}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})\mu\sqrt{R_{T,\delta}}}{(s+\gamma)\sqrt{d_{\mathsf{eff}}(T+\gamma)}}+\frac{4\kappa C\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})\sqrt{\alpha R_{T,\delta}}}{(s+\gamma)^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\mu\sqrt{R_{T,\delta}}\left(\frac{2\kappa\sqrt{C}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}{(s+\gamma)\sqrt{d_{\mathsf{eff}}(T+\gamma)}}+\frac{4\kappa C\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})\sqrt{\alpha}}{\mu(s+\gamma)^{2}}\right)

Bounding Ⓒ

By equation (
8
),

\displaystyle\frac{\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{s})\|^{3}\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{s}\right\}}{\Gamma^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\mu\sqrt{R_{T,\delta}}\cdot\frac{8\kappa^{3}C^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{2}}{(s+\gamma)^{3}}

Bounding Ⓓ

Since
\beta d\leq\tfrac{\mu^{2}R_{T,\delta}}{T+\gamma}
, it follows tat

\displaystyle\frac{\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{s})\|\|\Sigma_{s}\|_{2}d_{\mathsf{eff}}\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{s}\right\}}{\Gamma^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\kappa\sqrt{C}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{2}}{\mu\sqrt{R_{T,\delta}}(s+\gamma)}\left(\beta d+\frac{4\alpha CR_{T,\delta}d}{(s+\gamma)^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\kappa\sqrt{C}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{2}\mu\sqrt{R_{T,\delta}}}{(s+\gamma)(T+\gamma)}+\frac{8\alpha\kappa d\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{2}C^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}\sqrt{R_{T,\delta}}}{\mu(s+\gamma)^{3}}

\displaystyle\leq\mu\sqrt{R_{T,\delta}}\left(\frac{2\kappa\sqrt{C}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{2}}{(s+\gamma)(T+\gamma)}+\frac{8\alpha\kappa d\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{2}C^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}}{\mu^{2}(s+\gamma)^{3}}\right)

Hence, we conclude that

\displaystyle\|\tilde{\mathbf{b}}_{t}\|\leq\textcircled{A}+\textcircled{B}+\textcircled{C}+\textcircled{D}\leq\mu\sqrt{R_{T,\delta}}\sum_{j=1}^{7}B_{j}

where
B_{1},\dots,B_{7}
are defined as follows:

\displaystyle B_{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{T+\gamma},

\displaystyle B_{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{4\alpha C\sqrt{d}\ln(\nicefrac{{\ln(T)}}{{\delta}})}{\mu^{2}(s+\gamma)^{2}},

\displaystyle B_{3}

\displaystyle=\frac{2\kappa\sqrt{C}\ln(\nicefrac{{\ln(T)}}{{\delta}})}{(s+\gamma)\sqrt{d(T+\gamma)}},

\displaystyle B_{4}

\displaystyle=\frac{4\kappa C\ln(\nicefrac{{\ln(T)}}{{\delta}})\sqrt{\alpha}}{\mu(s+\gamma)^{2}},

\displaystyle B_{5}

\displaystyle=\frac{8\kappa^{3}C^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}\ln(\nicefrac{{\ln(T)}}{{\delta}})^{2}}{(s+\gamma)^{3}},

\displaystyle B_{6}

\displaystyle=\frac{2\kappa\sqrt{C}\ln(\nicefrac{{\ln(T)}}{{\delta}})^{2}}{(s+\gamma)(T+\gamma)},

\displaystyle B_{7}

\displaystyle=\frac{8\alpha\kappa d\ln(\nicefrac{{\ln(T)}}{{\delta}})^{2}C^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}}{\mu^{2}(s+\gamma)^{3}}

C.3
Proof of Lemma
9

As before, for
s\in[1:T]
define
\mathbb{E}[\tilde{\mathbf{v}}_{s}\tilde{\mathbf{v}}^{T}_{s}|\mathcal{F}_{s-1}]=\tilde{\Sigma}_{s}
. Following the same steps as in that of the proof of Lemma
7
, we use Lemma
4
and the fact that
\mathsf{Cov}[\mathbf{g}_{t}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}]=\Sigma_{t}
to obtain:

\displaystyle\|\tilde{\Sigma}_{s}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|\Sigma_{s}\|_{2}\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{s}\right\}+\frac{\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{s})\|^{4}\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{s}\right\}}{\Gamma^{2}}+\frac{\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{s})\|^{2}\mathsf{Tr}(\Sigma_{s})\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{s}\right\}}{\Gamma^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{t}\right\}\left(\beta+\alpha D^{2}_{s}\right)+\frac{\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{s})\|^{4}\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{s}\right\}}{\Gamma^{2}}+\frac{\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{s}\right\}\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{s})\|^{2}d_{\mathsf{eff}}}{\Gamma^{2}}\left(\beta+\alpha D^{2}_{s}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\beta+\frac{4\alpha CR_{T,\delta}}{(s+\gamma)^{2}}+\frac{\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{s})\|^{4}\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{s}\right\}}{\Gamma^{2}}+\frac{\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{t})\|^{2}d_{\mathsf{eff}}\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{s}\right\}}{\Gamma^{2}}\left(\beta+\frac{4\alpha CR_{T,\delta}}{(s+\gamma)^{2}}\right)

(17)

where the second inequality follows from Assumption
QG

2
nd

Moment
and the second inequality follows by definition of
E_{s}

Furthermore, since
\mathsf{clip}_{\Gamma}
is a convex projection, the following holds:

\displaystyle\mathsf{Tr}(\tilde{\Sigma}_{s})

\displaystyle\leq\mathsf{Tr}(\Sigma_{s})\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{s}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq d_{\mathsf{eff}}\left(\beta+\alpha D^{2}_{s}\right)\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{s}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\beta d_{\mathsf{eff}}+\frac{4\alpha dCR_{T,\delta}}{(s+\gamma)^{2}}

(18)

Now, for
s\in[t]
, we define
h_{s}
as follows:

\displaystyle h_{s}=\left\langle\tilde{\mathbf{v}}_{s},\mathbf{d}_{s}\right\rangle\frac{(s+\gamma)^{2A-1}}{(t+\gamma)^{2A-2}}

Note that
\mathbb{E}[h_{s}|\mathcal{F}_{s-1}]=0
. Furthermore, since
\|\tilde{\mathbf{v}}_{s}\|\leq 2\Gamma
and
\|\mathbf{d}_{s}\|\leq\tfrac{\sqrt{CR_{T,\delta}}}{s+\gamma-1}

\displaystyle|h_{s}|

\displaystyle\leq 2\Gamma\cdot\frac{\sqrt{CR_{T,\delta}}}{s+\gamma-1}\cdot\frac{(s+\gamma)^{2A-1}}{(t+\gamma)^{2A-2}}

\displaystyle\leq 4\Gamma\sqrt{CR_{T,\delta}}\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{2A-2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4\mu R_{T,\delta}\sqrt{C}}{\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}

(19)

For
s\in[t]
, define
\sigma^{2}_{s}=\mathbb{E}[h^{2}_{s}|\mathcal{F}_{s-1}]
. It follows that,

\displaystyle\sigma^{2}_{s}

\displaystyle=\frac{(s+\gamma)^{4A-2}}{(t+\gamma)^{4A-4}}\mathbf{v}^{T}_{s}\tilde{\Sigma}_{s}\mathbf{v}_{s}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(s+\gamma)^{4A-2}}{(t+\gamma)^{4A-4}}\|\mathbf{v}_{s}\|^{2}\|\tilde{\Sigma}_{s}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq 4CR_{T,\delta}\cdot\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{4A-4}\|\tilde{\Sigma}_{s}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq 4CR_{T,\delta}\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{4A-4}\left[\beta+\frac{4\alpha CR_{T,\delta}}{(s+\gamma)^{2}}+\frac{\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{s})\|^{4}\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{s}\right\}}{\Gamma^{2}}+\frac{\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{t})\|^{2}d_{\mathsf{eff}}\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{s}\right\}}{\Gamma^{2}}\left(\beta+\frac{4\alpha CR_{T,\delta}}{(s+\gamma)^{2}}\right)\right]

where the last inequality follows from equation (
9
) and the fact that
d_{\mathsf{eff}}=\nicefrac{{\mathsf{Tr}(\Sigma)}}{{\|\Sigma\|_{2}}}
. We now use the above inequality to control
\sum_{s=1}^{t}\sigma^{2}_{s}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})
as follows:

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}\sigma^{2}_{s}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})

\displaystyle\leq 4CR_{T,\delta}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{4A-4}\beta

\displaystyle+4CR_{T,\delta}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})\sum_{s=1}^{t}\frac{(s+\gamma)^{4A-6}}{(t+\gamma)^{4A-4}}4\alpha CR_{T,\delta}

\displaystyle+4CR_{T,\delta}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{4A-4}\frac{\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{s})\|^{4}\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{s}\right\}}{\Gamma^{2}}

\displaystyle+4CR_{T,\delta}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{4A-4}\frac{\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{s})\|^{2}\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{s}\right\}\beta d_{\mathsf{eff}}}{\Gamma^{2}}

\displaystyle+4CR_{T,\delta}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})\sum_{s=1}^{t}\frac{(s+\gamma)^{4A-6}}{(t+\gamma)^{4A-4}}\frac{4\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{s})\|^{2}\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{s}\right\}\alpha dCR_{T,\delta}}{\Gamma^{2}}

(20)

We now control each of the five terms in the above inequality as follows

\displaystyle 4CR_{T,\delta}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{4A-4}\beta

\displaystyle\leq 4CR_{T,\delta}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})\beta t

\displaystyle\leq 4CtR_{T,\delta}\cdot\frac{\mu^{2}R_{T,\delta}}{(T+\gamma)\sqrt{d_{\mathsf{eff}}}}

\displaystyle\leq 4\mu^{2}CR_{T,\delta}^{2}

To control the second term,

\displaystyle 4CR_{T,\delta}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})\sum_{s=1}^{t}\frac{(s+\gamma)^{4A-6}}{(t+\gamma)^{4A-4}}4\alpha CR_{T,\delta}

\displaystyle\leq 16CR_{T,\delta}^{2}\mu^{2}\frac{\alpha C\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}{\mu^{2}(t+\gamma)}

\displaystyle\leq 16CR_{T,\delta}^{2}\mu^{2}

where the second inequality follows by setting
A\geq\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}
and the last inequality follows by setting
\gamma\geq\frac{\alpha C\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}{\mu^{2}}

Before controlling the remaining terms, we recall from (
8
) in the proof of Lemma
6
that

\displaystyle\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{s})\|\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{s}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\kappa\Gamma\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})\sqrt{C}}{s+\gamma-1}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\kappa\Gamma\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})\sqrt{C}}{s+\gamma}

where
\Gamma=\tfrac{\mu\sqrt{R_{T,\delta}}}{\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}
. It follows that

\displaystyle\frac{\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{s})\|^{4}\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{s}\right\}}{\Gamma^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{16\kappa^{4}C^{2}\Gamma^{2}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{4}}{(s+\gamma)^{4}}

\displaystyle=\mu^{2}R_{T,\delta}\cdot\frac{16\kappa^{4}C^{2}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{2}}{(s+\gamma)^{4}}

Thus, we can control the third term in equation (
20
) as follows

\displaystyle 4CR_{T,\delta}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{4A-4}\frac{\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{s})\|^{4}\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{s}\right\}}{\Gamma^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq 64\mu^{2}CR_{T,\delta}^{2}\cdot\kappa^{4}C^{2}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{3}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\frac{(s+\gamma)^{4A-8}}{(t+\gamma)^{4A-4}}

\displaystyle\leq 64\mu^{2}CR_{T,\delta}^{2}\cdot\frac{\kappa^{4}C^{2}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{3}}{(t+\gamma)^{3}}

\displaystyle\leq 64\mu^{2}CR_{T,\delta}^{2}

where the second inequality follows by setting
A\geq 2
and the last inequality follows by setting
\gamma\geq\kappa^{\nicefrac{{4}}{{3}}}C^{\nicefrac{{2}}{{3}}}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})
.

To control the fourth term in (
20
), we note that by equation (
8
) and the definition of
R_{T,\delta}

\displaystyle\frac{\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{s})\|^{2}d_{\mathsf{eff}}\beta\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{s}\right\}}{\Gamma^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq 4\mu^{2}R_{T,\delta}\cdot\frac{\kappa^{2}C\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{2}}{(T+\gamma)(s+\gamma)^{2}}

It follows that

\displaystyle 4CR_{T,\delta}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{4A-4}\frac{\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{s})\|^{2}\beta d_{\mathsf{eff}}}{\Gamma^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq 16\mu^{2}CR_{T,\delta}^{2}\cdot\frac{\kappa^{2}C\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{3}}{T+\gamma}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\frac{(s+\gamma)^{4A-6}}{(t+\gamma)^{4A-4}}

\displaystyle\leq 16\mu^{2}CR_{T,\delta}^{2}\cdot\frac{\kappa^{2}C\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{3}}{(T+\gamma)(t+\gamma)}

\displaystyle\leq 16\mu^{2}CR_{T,\delta}^{2}

where the second inequality follows by setting
A\geq\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}
and the last inequality follows by setting
\gamma\geq\kappa\sqrt{C}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}
.

To control the fifth term in equation (
20
), we proceed as follows:

\displaystyle 4CR_{T,\delta}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})\sum_{s=1}^{t}\frac{(s+\gamma)^{4A-6}}{(t+\gamma)^{4A-4}}\frac{4\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{s})\|^{2}\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{s}\right\}\alpha dCR_{T,\delta}}{\Gamma^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq 64\mu^{2}CR_{T,\delta}^{2}\frac{\alpha d\kappa^{2}C^{2}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{3}}{\mu^{2}}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\frac{(s+\gamma)^{4A-8}}{(t+\gamma)^{4A-4}}

\displaystyle\leq 64\mu^{2}CR_{T,\delta}^{2}\cdot\frac{\alpha d\kappa^{2}C^{2}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{3}}{\mu^{2}(t+\gamma)^{3}}

\displaystyle\leq 64\mu^{2}CR_{T,\delta}^{2}

where the second inequality follows by setting
A\geq 2
and the last inequality follows by setting
\gamma\geq\frac{\alpha^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{3}}}d_{\mathsf{eff}}^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{3}}}\kappa^{\nicefrac{{2}}{{3}}}C^{\nicefrac{{2}}{{3}}}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}{\mu^{\nicefrac{{2}}{{3}}}}

Substituting the above bounds into equation (
20
), we note that

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}\sigma^{2}_{s}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})\leq 164\mu^{2}CR_{T,\delta}

Thus, by Freedman’s inequality (Lemma
3
), we conclude that the following holds with probability at least
1-\nicefrac{{\delta}}{{2}}
uniformly for every
t\in[T]
:

\sum_{s=1}^{t}\frac{(s+\gamma)^{2A-1}}{(t+\gamma)^{2A-2}}\left\langle\tilde{\mathbf{v}}_{s},\mathbf{d}_{s}\right\rangle=\sum_{s=1}^{t}h_{s}\leq 2\sqrt{\sum_{s=1}^{t}\sigma^{2}_{s}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}+8\mu R_{T,\delta}\sqrt{C}\leq 34R_{T,\delta}\sqrt{C}

(21)

To prove the second inequality of this lemma, we define
\mathbf{z}_{s}=\tilde{\mathbf{v}}_{s}\cdot\left(\tfrac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{A-1}
for
s\in[t]
. Note that
\mathbb{E}[\mathbf{z}_{s}|\mathcal{F}_{s-1}]=0
and
\|\mathbf{z}_{s}\|\leq\|\tilde{\mathbf{v}}_{s}\|\leq 2\Gamma
. Define the PSD matrices
\mathbf{G}_{s}=\mathbb{E}[\mathbf{z}_{s}\mathbf{z}^{T}_{s}|\mathcal{F}_{s-1}]=\left(\tfrac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{2A-2}\tilde{\Sigma}_{s}
. Recalling the bounds obtained on
\|\tilde{\Sigma}_{s}\|_{2}
and
\mathsf{Tr}(\tilde{\Sigma}_{s})
in equations (
17
) and (
18
), we infer the following:

\displaystyle\mathsf{Tr}(\mathbf{G}_{s})

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{2A-2}\mathsf{Tr}(\Sigma_{s})\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{s}\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{2A-2}\beta d_{\mathsf{eff}}+\frac{(s+\gamma)^{2A-4}}{(t+\gamma)^{2A-2}}4\alpha d_{\mathsf{eff}}CR_{T,\delta}

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{G}_{s}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{2A-2}\|\tilde{\Sigma}_{s}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{2A-2}\beta+\frac{(s+\gamma)^{2A-4}}{(t+\gamma)^{2A-2}}4\alpha CR_{T,\delta}+\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{2A-2}\frac{\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{s})\|^{4}\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{s}\right\}}{\Gamma^{2}}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{2A-2}\frac{\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{s})\|^{2}\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{s}\right\}\beta d_{\mathsf{eff}}}{\Gamma^{2}}+\frac{(s+\gamma)^{2A-4}}{(t+\gamma)^{2A-2}}\frac{\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{s})\|^{2}\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{s}\right\}4\alpha d_{\mathsf{eff}}CR_{T,\delta}}{\Gamma^{2}}

Substituting equation (
8
) into the bound for
\|\mathbf{G}_{s}\|_{2}
, we obtain the following

\displaystyle\mathsf{Tr}(\mathbf{G}_{s})

\displaystyle\leq q_{s}=\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{2A-2}\beta d_{\mathsf{eff}}+\frac{(s+\gamma)^{2A-4}}{(t+\gamma)^{2A-2}}4\alpha d_{\mathsf{eff}}CR_{T,\delta}

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{G}_{s}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq p_{s}=\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{2A-2}\beta+\frac{(s+\gamma)^{2A-4}}{(t+\gamma)^{2A-2}}\cdot 4\alpha CR_{T,\delta}+\frac{(s+\gamma)^{2A-6}}{(t+\gamma)^{2A-2}}\cdot 16\kappa^{4}C^{2}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{2}\mu^{2}R_{T,\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(s+\gamma)^{2A-4}}{(t+\gamma)^{2A-2}}\cdot 4\beta d_{\mathsf{eff}}\kappa^{2}C\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{2}+\frac{(s+\gamma)^{2A-6}}{(t+\gamma)^{2A-2}}\cdot 16\alpha d_{\mathsf{eff}}R_{T,\delta}\kappa^{2}C^{2}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{2}

(22)

By Cauchy Schwarz inequality,

\displaystyle p^{2}_{s}

\displaystyle\leq 5\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{4A-4}\beta^{2}+5\cdot\frac{(s+\gamma)^{4A-8}}{(t+\gamma)^{4A-4}}16\alpha^{2}C^{2}R_{T,\delta}^{2}+5\cdot\frac{(s+\gamma)^{4A-12}}{(t+\gamma)^{4A-4}}\cdot 256\kappa^{8}C^{4}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{4}\mu^{4}R_{T,\delta}^{2}

\displaystyle+5\cdot\frac{(s+\gamma)^{4A-8}}{(t+\gamma)^{4A-4}}\cdot 16\beta^{2}d_{\mathsf{eff}}^{2}\kappa^{4}C^{2}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{4}+5\cdot\frac{(s+\gamma)^{4A-12}}{(t+\gamma)^{4A-4}}\cdot 256\alpha^{2}d_{\mathsf{eff}}^{2}R_{T,\delta}^{2}\kappa^{4}C^{4}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{4}

(23)

Since
T\gtrsim\ln(\ln(d))
,
K=\ln(\ln(T))
, our choice of
\Gamma
and the definition of
R_{T,\delta}
ensures that the conditions of Corollary
5
are satisfied. Hence, by Corollary
5
, we conclude that the following holds with probability
1-\nicefrac{{\delta}}{{2}}
uniformly for all
t\in[T]

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}\|\mathbf{z}_{s}\|^{2}\leq 4C_{M}\Gamma^{2}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{2}+C_{M}\sum_{s=1}^{\mathsf{UP}(t)}Q_{s}+\frac{C_{M}t}{4\Gamma^{2}}\sum_{s=1}^{t}P^{2}_{s}

Simplyfing the above using equations (
22
), (
23
) and the definition of
\Gamma
, we obtain the following:

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}\|\mathbf{z}_{s}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 4C_{M}\mu^{2}R_{T,\delta}+C_{M}\sum_{s=1}^{\mathsf{UP}(t)}\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{2A-2}\beta d_{\mathsf{eff}}+C_{M}\sum_{s=1}^{\mathsf{UP}(t)}\frac{(s+\gamma)^{2A-4}}{(t+\gamma)^{2A-2}}\cdot 4\alpha d_{\mathsf{eff}}CR_{T,\delta}

\displaystyle+\frac{5C_{M}}{4}\sum_{s=1}^{\mathsf{UP}(t)}\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{4A-4}\frac{\beta^{2}t\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{2}}{\mu^{2}R_{T,\delta}}+\frac{5C_{M}}{4}\sum_{s=1}^{\mathsf{UP}(t)}\frac{(s+\gamma)^{4A-8}}{(t+\gamma)^{4A-4}}\cdot\frac{16\alpha^{2}C^{2}R_{T,\delta}t\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{2}}{\mu^{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{5C_{M}}{4}\sum_{s=1}^{\mathsf{UP}(t)}\frac{(s+\gamma)^{4A-12}}{(t+\gamma)^{4A-4}}\cdot 256\kappa^{8}C^{4}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{6}t\mu^{2}R_{T,\delta}

\displaystyle+\frac{5C_{M}}{4}\sum_{s=1}^{\mathsf{UP}(t)}\frac{(s+\gamma)^{4A-8}}{(t+\gamma)^{4A-4}}\cdot\frac{\beta^{2}d_{\mathsf{eff}}^{2}t}{\mu^{2}R_{T,\delta}}\cdot 16\kappa^{4}C^{2}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{6}

\displaystyle+\frac{5C_{M}}{4}\sum_{s=1}^{\mathsf{UP}(t)}\frac{(s+\gamma)^{4A-12}}{(t+\gamma)^{4A-4}}\cdot\frac{256\kappa^{4}C^{4}\alpha^{2}d_{\mathsf{eff}}^{2}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{6}R_{T,\delta}}{\mu^{2}}

(24)

We now simplify each term in the above inequality by using the fact that
\mathsf{UP}(t)\leq\min\{T,2t\}
. To this end, the second term is simplified as follows by using
A\geq 1

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{\mathsf{UP}(t)}\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{4A-4}\beta d_{\mathsf{eff}}

\displaystyle\leq\mathsf{UP}(t)\beta d_{\mathsf{eff}}\leq\mu^{2}R_{T,\delta}

We now control the third term by noting that for
s\leq 2t
,
s+\gamma\leq 2t+\gamma\leq 2(t+\gamma)
:

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}\frac{(s+\gamma)^{2A-4}}{(t+\gamma)^{2A-2}}\cdot 4\alpha d_{\mathsf{eff}}CR_{T,\delta}

\displaystyle\leq\mu^{2}R_{T,\delta}\cdot\frac{2^{2A-2}\alpha d_{\mathsf{eff}}}{\mu^{2}}\sum_{s=1}^{2t}\frac{1}{(t+\gamma)^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq 2^{2A-1}\mu^{2}R_{T,\delta}\cdot\frac{\alpha dt}{\mu^{2}(t+\gamma)^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq 2^{2A-3}\mu^{2}R_{T,\delta}

where the last inquality follows by setting
\gamma\geq\frac{4\alpha Cd_{\mathsf{eff}}}{\mu^{2}}
.

We now control the fourth term as follows:

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{\mathsf{UP}(t)}\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{4A-4}\frac{\beta^{2}t\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{2}}{\mu^{2}R_{T,\delta}}

\displaystyle\leq\mu^{2}R_{T,\delta}\cdot\frac{\mathsf{UP}(t)}{d(T+\gamma)^{2}}\leq\mu^{2}R_{T,\delta}

We now control the fifth term as follows:

\displaystyle\frac{16\alpha^{2}C^{2}R_{T,\delta}t\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{2}}{\mu^{2}}\sum_{s=1}^{\mathsf{UP}(t)}\frac{(s+\gamma)^{4A-8}}{(t+\gamma)^{4A-4}}

\displaystyle\leq\mu^{2}R_{T,\delta}\cdot\frac{2^{4A-4}\alpha^{2}C^{2}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{2}t}{\mu^{4}}\sum_{s=1}^{2t}\frac{1}{(t+\gamma)^{4}}

\displaystyle\leq\mu^{2}R_{T,\delta}\cdot\frac{\alpha^{2}C^{2}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{2}2^{4A-5}}{\mu^{4}(t+\gamma)^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq 2^{4A-9}\mu^{2}R_{T,\delta}

where the last inequality uses
\gamma\geq\frac{4\alpha C\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}{\mu^{2}}

We now simplify the sixth term as follows:

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{\mathsf{UP}(t)}\frac{(s+\gamma)^{4A-12}}{(t+\gamma)^{4A-4}}\cdot 256\mu^{2}R_{T,\delta}\kappa^{8}C^{4}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{6}t

\displaystyle\leq\mu^{2}R_{T,\delta}t\cdot 2^{4A-4}\kappa^{8}C^{4}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{6}\sum_{s=1}^{2t}\frac{1}{(t+\gamma)^{8}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mu^{2}R_{T,\delta}}{(t+\gamma)^{6}}\cdot 2^{4A-3}\kappa^{8}C^{4}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{6}

\displaystyle\leq 2^{4A-15}\mu^{2}R_{T,\delta}

where the last inequality follows by setting
\gamma\geq 4\kappa^{\nicefrac{{4}}{{3}}}C^{\nicefrac{{2}}{{3}}}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})
.

We control the seventh term as follows:

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{\mathsf{UP}(t)}\frac{(s+\gamma)^{4A-8}}{(t+\gamma)^{4A-4}}\cdot\frac{\beta^{2}d_{\mathsf{eff}}^{2}t}{\mu^{2}R_{T,\delta}}\cdot 16\kappa^{4}C^{2}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{6}

\displaystyle\leq\mu^{2}R_{T,\delta}\cdot\frac{2^{4}\kappa^{4}C^{2}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{6}t}{(T+\gamma)^{2}}\sum_{s=1}^{2t}\frac{(s+\gamma)^{4A-8}}{(t+\gamma)^{4A-4}}

\displaystyle\leq\mu^{2}R_{T,\delta}\cdot\frac{2^{4A-3}\kappa^{4}C^{2}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{6}}{(t+\gamma)^{4}}

\displaystyle\leq 2^{4A-11}\mu^{2}R_{T,\delta}

where
\gamma\geq 4\kappa\sqrt{C}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}
.

We use a similar argument to simplify the final term as follows:

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{\mathsf{UP}(t)}\frac{t(s+\gamma)^{4A-12}}{(t+\gamma)^{4A-4}}\cdot\frac{2^{8}\alpha^{2}d_{\mathsf{eff}}^{2}R_{T,\delta}\kappa^{4}C^{4}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{6}}{\mu^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\mu^{2}R_{T,\delta}\cdot\frac{2^{4A-3}\alpha^{2}d_{\mathsf{eff}}^{2}\kappa^{4}C^{4}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{6}}{\mu^{4}(t+\gamma)^{6}}

\displaystyle\leq 2^{4A-15}\mu^{2}R_{T,\delta}

where
\gamma\geq\frac{4\kappa^{\nicefrac{{2}}{{3}}}d_{\mathsf{eff}}^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{3}}}C^{\nicefrac{{2}}{{3}}}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}{\mu^{\nicefrac{{2}}{{3}}}}
.
We now set
A\geq 3
and
\gamma
as follows:

\displaystyle\gamma\geq 4C\max\{\frac{\alpha d_{\mathsf{eff}}}{\mu^{2}},\frac{\alpha\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}{\mu^{2}},\kappa^{\nicefrac{{4}}{{3}}}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}}),\kappa\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}},\frac{\kappa^{\nicefrac{{2}}{{3}}}d_{\mathsf{eff}}^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{3}}}\alpha^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{3}}}}{\mu^{\nicefrac{{2}}{{3}}}}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})\}

Under these parameter settings, we substitute the obtained bounds into equation (
15
), we conclude that the following holds with probability at least
1-\nicefrac{{\delta}}{{2}}
uniformly for every
t\in[T]
:

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(\frac{s+\gamma}{t+\gamma}\right)^{2A-2}\|\tilde{\mathbf{v}}_{s}\|^{2}=\sum_{s=1}^{t}\|\mathbf{z}_{s}\|^{2}\leq C_{M}\mu^{2}R_{T,\delta}\left(2^{4A-3}\frac{25}{4}+5\cdot 2^{4A-11}+5\cdot 2^{4A-16}+5\cdot 2^{4A-13}\right)

The proof is completed via a union bound.

Appendix D
Analysis for Smooth Convex Functions

Let
d_{\mathsf{eff}}=\tfrac{\mathsf{Tr}(\Sigma)}{\|\Sigma\|_{2}}
. Following a convention similar to that of Section
B
, let
K=4\max\{8,C_{M},\ln(T)\}
. For
t\geq 1
, define the filtration
\mathcal{F}_{t}=\sigma\left(\mathbf{x}_{1},\mathbf{g}_{s}|1\leq s\leq t\right)
and
\mathcal{F}_{0}=\sigma(\mathbf{x}_{1})
. Furthermore, let
\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{t})=\mathsf{clip}_{\Gamma}(\mathbf{g}_{t})+\mathbf{b}_{t}+\mathbf{v}_{t}
where

\mathbf{b}_{t}=\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{t})-\mathbb{E}[\mathsf{clip}_{\Gamma}(\mathbf{g}_{t})|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}]
and
\mathbf{v}_{t}=\mathbb{E}[\mathsf{clip}_{\Gamma}(\mathbf{g}_{t})|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}]-\mathsf{clip}_{\Gamma}(\mathbf{g}_{t})
. As beforem, we note that
\mathbb{E}[\mathbf{v}_{t}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}]=0
and
\|\mathbf{v}_{t}\|\leq 2\Gamma
. Hence
\mathbf{v}_{t}
is an
\mathcal{F}
adapted almost surely bounded martingale difference sequence. Now, let
D_{t}=\|\mathbf{x}_{t}-\mathbf{x}^{*}\|
where
\mathbf{x}^{*}
is the minimizer of
F
considered in the statement of Theorem
3
. Using the smoothness and convexity properties of
F
, we first prove the following intermediate average iterate guarantee:

Lemma 10

(Intermediate Average Iterate Guarantee)
.

The following holds for
\eta\leq\nicefrac{{1}}{{2L}}

\displaystyle F(\hat{\mathbf{x}}_{T})-F(\mathbf{x}^{*})\leq\frac{D^{2}_{1}}{2\eta T}+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\langle\mathbf{b}_{t},\mathbf{x}_{t}-\mathbf{x}^{*}\right\rangle+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\langle\mathbf{v}_{t},\mathbf{x}_{t}-\mathbf{x}^{*}\right\rangle+\frac{2\eta}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\|\mathbf{b}_{t}\|^{2}+\frac{2\eta}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\|\mathbf{v}_{t}\|^{2}

Define the events
E_{t}
and the random vectors
\mathbf{d}_{t}
,
\tilde{\mathbf{b}}_{t}
and
\tilde{\mathbf{v}}_{t}
as follows for
t\in[T]
:

\displaystyle E_{t}

\displaystyle=\{D_{t}\leq 2D_{1}\}

\displaystyle\mathbf{d}_{t}

\displaystyle=(\mathbf{x}_{t}-\mathbf{x}^{*})\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{t}\right\}

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathbf{b}}_{t}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{b}_{t}\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{t}\right\}

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathbf{v}}_{t}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{v}_{t}\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{t}\right\}

We use the following lemma to control the bias

Lemma 11

(Bias Control)
.

For every
t\in[T]
,
\|\tilde{\mathbf{b}}_{t}\|\leq B
where
B
is defined as follows:

\displaystyle B

\displaystyle=\frac{\|\Sigma\|_{2}\sqrt{d_{\mathsf{eff}}}}{\Gamma}+\frac{2LD_{1}\sqrt{\|\Sigma\|_{2}}}{\Gamma}+\frac{8L^{3}D^{3}_{1}}{\Gamma^{2}}+\frac{2\|\Sigma\|_{2}d_{\mathsf{eff}}LD_{1}}{\Gamma^{2}}

We use the following lemma to control the varince

Lemma 12

(Variance Control)
.

Let
V\geq 0
be defined as follows:

\displaystyle V

\displaystyle=\|\Sigma\|_{2}+\frac{16L^{4}D^{4}_{1}}{\Gamma^{2}}+\frac{4L^{2}D^{2}_{1}\|\Sigma\|_{2}d_{\mathsf{eff}}}{\Gamma^{2}}

Then the following holds with probability at least
1-\delta
uniformly for every
t\in[T]

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left\langle\tilde{\mathbf{v}}_{t},\mathbf{d}_{t}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq 4D_{1}\sqrt{Vt\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}+8\Gamma D_{1}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}\|\tilde{\mathbf{v}}_{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{M}g^{2}T

where
C_{M}
is a numerical constant and
g^{2}
is defined as follows

\displaystyle g^{2}

\displaystyle=\|\Sigma\|_{2}d_{\mathsf{eff}}+\frac{4\Gamma^{2}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{2}}{T}+\frac{V^{2}T}{4\Gamma^{2}}

Let
E
denote the following event

\displaystyle E=\{

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left\langle\tilde{\mathbf{v}}_{s},\mathbf{d}_{s}\right\rangle\leq 4D_{1}\sqrt{Vt\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}+8\Gamma D_{1}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})\quad\forall\ t\in[T]

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}\|\tilde{\mathbf{v}}_{s}\|^{2}\leq C_{M}g^{2}T\quad\forall t\in[T]\}

We define the constant
A
as follows:

\displaystyle A=\|\Sigma\|_{2}\sqrt{d_{\mathsf{eff}}}+LD_{1}\sqrt{\|\Sigma\|_{2}}=\sqrt{\|\Sigma\|_{2}}\left(\sqrt{\mathsf{Tr}(\Sigma)}+LD_{1}\right)

We now set the clipping level
\Gamma=\sqrt{\tfrac{AT}{\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}}
. For this choice of
\Gamma
, we now obtain the following bound on
B
:

\displaystyle B

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\|\Sigma\|_{2}\sqrt{d_{\mathsf{eff}}}}{\Gamma}+\frac{2LD_{1}\sqrt{\|\Sigma\|_{2}}}{\Gamma}+\frac{8L^{3}D^{3}_{1}}{\Gamma^{2}}+\frac{2\|\Sigma\|_{2}d_{\mathsf{eff}}LD_{1}}{\Gamma^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq 2\sqrt{\frac{A\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}{T}}+\frac{2LD_{1}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}{AT}\left(\|\Sigma\|_{2}d_{\mathsf{eff}}+L^{2}D^{2}_{1}\right)=B^{\prime}

(25)

Similarly, we bound the value of
V
as follows:

\displaystyle V

\displaystyle\leq\|\Sigma\|_{2}+\frac{16L^{4}D^{4}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}{AT}+\frac{4L^{2}D^{2}\|\Sigma\|_{2}d\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}{AT}=V^{\prime}

(26)

Equipped with the above inequality, we then bound the value of
g
as:

\displaystyle g

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\|\Sigma\|_{2}d_{\mathsf{eff}}}+\frac{2\Gamma\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}{\sqrt{T}}+\frac{V\sqrt{T}}{2\Gamma}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\|\Sigma\|_{2}d_{\mathsf{eff}}}+2\sqrt{A\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}+\frac{V\sqrt{\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}}{2\sqrt{A}}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\|\Sigma\|_{2}d_{\mathsf{eff}}}+2\sqrt{A\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}+\frac{\|\Sigma\|_{2}\sqrt{\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}}{2\sqrt{A}}+\frac{8L^{4}D^{4}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}}{A^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}T}+\frac{2L^{2}D^{2}\|\Sigma\|_{2}d_{\mathsf{eff}}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}}{A^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}T}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\|\Sigma\|_{2}d_{\mathsf{eff}}}+3\sqrt{A\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}+\frac{8L^{4}D^{4}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}}{A^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}T}+\frac{2L^{2}D^{2}\|\Sigma\|_{2}d_{\mathsf{eff}}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}}{A^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}T}=g^{\prime}

(27)

We prove the following lemma to control the growth of the iterates
D_{t}
.

Lemma 13

(Iterate Bound)
.

Let
\eta\leq c\min\{\nicefrac{{1}}{{2L}},\nicefrac{{D_{1}}}{{B^{\prime}T}},\nicefrac{{D_{1}}}{{g^{\prime}\sqrt{T}}}\}
where
c=\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{8C_{M}+330}}
. Then, conditioned on the event
E
,
D_{t}\leq 2D_{1}\ \forall t\in[T]
.

Equipped with the above lemmas, we now present a proof of the following theorem, which is a formal restatement of Theorem
3

Theorem 7

(Smooth Convex Objectives)
.

Let
Convexity
,

L

-smoothness
and
Bdd.

2
nd

Moment
be satisfied. Then, for any
\delta\in(0,\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}})
and
T\geq\ln(\ln(d))
, there exists an
\eta\in(0,\nicefrac{{1}}{{2L}}]
such that the average iterate of Algorithm
1
run for
T
iterations with step-size
\eta_{t}=\eta
and clipping level
\Gamma=\sqrt{\tfrac{T\sqrt{\|\Sigma\|_{2}}(\sqrt{\mathsf{Tr}(\Sigma)}+LD_{1})}{\ln(\nicefrac{{\ln(T)}}{{\delta}})}}
satisfies the following with probability at least
1-\delta
:

\displaystyle F(\hat{\mathbf{x}}_{T})-F(\mathbf{x}^{*})

\displaystyle\lesssim D_{1}\sqrt{\frac{\mathsf{Tr}(\Sigma)+\sqrt{\|\Sigma\|_{2}}\left(\sqrt{\mathsf{Tr}(\Sigma)}+LD_{1}\right)\ln(\nicefrac{{\ln(T)}}{{\delta}})}{T}}+\frac{LD^{2}_{1}}{T}

\displaystyle+\frac{LD^{2}_{1}\ln(\nicefrac{{\ln(T)}}{{\delta}})}{T}\sqrt{\frac{\mathsf{Tr}(\Sigma)+L^{2}D^{2}_{1}}{\|\Sigma\|_{2}}}+\frac{L^{2}D^{3}_{1}\ln(\nicefrac{{\ln(T)}}{{\delta}})^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}}{T^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}}\left[\frac{\mathsf{Tr}(\Sigma)+L^{2}D^{2}_{1}}{\|\Sigma\|^{3}}\right]^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{4}}}

D.1
Proof of Theorem
7

We condition on the event
E
and let
\eta=\tfrac{c}{2}\min\{\tfrac{1}{2L},\tfrac{D_{1}}{B^{\prime}T},\tfrac{D_{1}}{g^{\prime}\sqrt{T}}\}
where
c=\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{8C_{M}+330}}
. Note that this choice of
\eta
satisfies the requirements of Lemma
10
and Lemma
13
. By Lemma
10
, the following holds:

\displaystyle F(\hat{\mathbf{x}}_{T})-F(\mathbf{x}^{*})\leq\frac{D^{2}_{1}}{2\eta T}+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\langle\mathbf{b}_{t},\mathbf{x}_{t}-\mathbf{x}^{*}\right\rangle+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\langle\mathbf{v}_{t},\mathbf{x}_{t}-\mathbf{x}^{*}\right\rangle+\frac{2\eta}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\|\mathbf{b}_{t}\|^{2}+\frac{2\eta}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\|\mathbf{v}_{t}\|^{2}

By Lemma
13
,
\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{t}\right\}=1\ \forall t\in[T]
. Hence, the following holds.

\displaystyle F(\hat{\mathbf{x}}_{T})-F(\mathbf{x}^{*})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{D^{2}_{1}}{2\eta T}+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\langle\tilde{\mathbf{b}}_{t},\mathbf{d}_{t}\right\rangle+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\langle\tilde{\mathbf{v}}_{t},\mathbf{d}_{t}\right\rangle+\frac{2\eta}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\|\tilde{\mathbf{b}}_{t}\|^{2}+\frac{2\eta}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\|\tilde{\mathbf{v}}_{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{D^{2}_{1}}{2\eta T}+2BD_{1}+2\eta B^{2}+2\eta C_{M}g^{2}+4D_{1}\sqrt{\frac{V\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}{T}}+\frac{8D_{1}\Gamma\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}{T}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{D^{2}_{1}}{\eta T}+3\eta B^{2}T+2\eta C_{M}g^{2}+4D_{1}\sqrt{\frac{V\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}{T}}+8D_{1}\sqrt{\frac{A\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}{T}}

Where the second inequality uses Lemma
11
and the definition of the event
E
and the third inequality uses
ab\leq a^{2}+\nicefrac{{b^{2}}}{{4}}
. For the rest of the proof, we shall use
C
to denote an absolute numerical constant whose value can differ at every step. By our choice of the step-size

\displaystyle\frac{D^{2}}{\eta T}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{CLD^{2}_{1}}{T}+CD_{1}B^{\prime}+\frac{CD_{1}g^{\prime}}{\sqrt{T}}

\displaystyle 3\eta B^{2}T

\displaystyle\leq CD_{1}B^{\prime}

\displaystyle 2\eta C_{M}g^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{CD_{1}g^{\prime}}{\sqrt{T}}

Hence, conditioned on the event
E
, the following holds:

\displaystyle F(\hat{\mathbf{x}}_{T})-F(\mathbf{x}^{*})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{CLD^{2}_{1}}{T}+CD_{1}B^{\prime}+CD_{1}g^{\prime}\sqrt{T}+CD_{1}\sqrt{\frac{V^{\prime}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}{T}}+CD_{1}\sqrt{\frac{A\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}{T}}

Substituting the values of
g^{\prime},B^{\prime}
and
V^{\prime}
, we obtain the following:

\displaystyle F(\hat{\mathbf{x}}_{T})-F(\mathbf{x}^{*})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{CLD^{2}_{1}}{T}+CD_{1}\sqrt{\frac{A\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}{T}}+\frac{CLD^{2}_{1}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}{T}\cdot\left[\frac{\mathsf{Tr}(\Sigma)+L^{2}D^{2}_{1}}{A}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{CLD^{2}_{1}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}{T}\cdot\sqrt{\frac{\mathsf{Tr}(\Sigma)+L^{2}D^{2}}{A}}+\frac{CL^{2}D^{3}_{1}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}}{T^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}}\cdot\left[\frac{\mathsf{Tr}(\Sigma)+L^{2}D^{2}_{1}}{A^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}}\right]

Substituting the value of
A
, we conclude that the following inequality holds almost surely conditioned on the event
E

\displaystyle F(\hat{\mathbf{x}}_{T})-F(\mathbf{x}^{*})

\displaystyle\lesssim D_{1}\sqrt{\frac{\mathsf{Tr}(\Sigma)+\sqrt{\|\Sigma\|_{2}}\left(\sqrt{\mathsf{Tr}(\Sigma)}+LD_{1}\right)\ln(\nicefrac{{\ln(T)}}{{\delta}})}{T}}+\frac{LD^{2}_{1}}{T}

\displaystyle+\frac{LD^{2}_{1}\ln(\nicefrac{{\ln(T)}}{{\delta}})}{T}\sqrt{\frac{\mathsf{Tr}(\Sigma)+L^{2}D^{2}_{1}}{\|\Sigma\|_{2}}}+\frac{L^{2}D^{3}_{1}\ln(\nicefrac{{\ln(T)}}{{\delta}})^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}}{T^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}}\left[\frac{\mathsf{Tr}(\Sigma)+L^{2}D^{2}_{1}}{\|\Sigma\|^{3}}\right]^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{4}}}

The prooof is completed by observing that
\mathbb{P}(E)\geq 1-\delta
by Lemma
12
which implies that the above inequality also holds with probability at least
1-\delta

D.2
Proof of Lemma
10

Proof.

Since
\Pi_{\mathcal{C}}
is a contractive operator

\displaystyle D^{2}_{t+1}

\displaystyle=\|\mathbf{x}_{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{*}\|^{2}\leq D^{2}_{t}-2\eta\left\langle\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{t})-\mathbf{b}_{t}-\mathbf{v}_{t},\mathbf{x}_{t}-\mathbf{x}^{*}\right\rangle+\eta^{2}\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{t})-\mathbf{b}_{t}-\mathbf{v}_{t}\|

\displaystyle\leq D^{2}_{t}-2\eta\left\langle\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{t}),\mathbf{x}_{t}-\mathbf{x}^{*}\right\rangle+2\eta\left\langle\mathbf{b}_{t},\mathbf{x}_{t}-\mathbf{x}^{*}\right\rangle+2\eta\left\langle\mathbf{v}_{t},\mathbf{x}_{t}-\mathbf{x}^{*}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+2\eta^{2}\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{t})\|+4\eta^{2}\|\mathbf{v}_{t}\|^{2}+4\eta^{2}\|\mathbf{b}_{t}\|^{2}

By the coercivity property,

\displaystyle-2\eta\left\langle\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{t}),\mathbf{x}_{t}-\mathbf{x}^{*}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq-2\eta[F(\mathbf{x}_{t})-F(\mathbf{x}^{*})]-\frac{\eta}{L}\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{t})\|^{2}

Substituting this into the recurrence for
D^{2}_{t+1}
, we obtain the following:

\displaystyle D^{2}_{t+1}

\displaystyle\leq D^{2}_{t}-2\eta[F(\mathbf{x}_{t})-F(\mathbf{x}^{*})]+2\eta\left\langle\mathbf{v}_{t},\mathbf{x}_{t}-\mathbf{x}^{*}\right\rangle+2\eta\left\langle\mathbf{b}_{t},\mathbf{x}_{t}-\mathbf{x}^{*}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\eta(2\eta-\nicefrac{{1}}{{L}})\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{t})\|^{2}+4\eta^{2}\|\mathbf{v}_{t}\|^{2}+4\eta^{2}\|\mathbf{b}_{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq D^{2}_{t}-2\eta[F(\mathbf{x}_{t})-F(\mathbf{x}^{*})]+2\eta\left\langle\mathbf{v}_{t},\mathbf{x}_{t}-\mathbf{x}^{*}\right\rangle+2\eta\left\langle\mathbf{b}_{t},\mathbf{x}_{t}-\mathbf{x}^{*}\right\rangle+4\eta^{2}\|\mathbf{v}_{t}\|^{2}+4\eta^{2}\|\mathbf{b}_{t}\|^{2}

where the last inequality uses the fact that
\eta\leq\nicefrac{{1}}{{2L}}
, Rearranging and taking averages on both sides

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}F(\mathbf{x}_{t})-F(\mathbf{x}^{*})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{D^{2}_{1}}{2\eta T}+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\langle\mathbf{b}_{t},\mathbf{x}_{t}-\mathbf{x}^{*}\right\rangle+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\langle\mathbf{v}_{t},\mathbf{x}_{t}-\mathbf{x}^{*}\right\rangle+\frac{2\eta}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\|\mathbf{b}_{t}\|^{2}+\frac{2\eta}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\|\mathbf{v}_{t}\|^{2}

Using the above inequality and the convexity of
F
, we conclude that

\displaystyle F(\hat{\mathbf{x}}_{T})-F(\mathbf{x}^{*})

\displaystyle=F\left(\tfrac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{x}_{t}\right)-F(\mathbf{x}^{*})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}F(\mathbf{x}_{t})-F(\mathbf{x}^{*})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{D^{2}_{1}}{2\eta T}+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\langle\mathbf{b}_{t},\mathbf{x}_{t}-\mathbf{x}^{*}\right\rangle+\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\langle\mathbf{v}_{t},\mathbf{x}_{t}-\mathbf{x}^{*}\right\rangle+\frac{2\eta}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\|\mathbf{b}_{t}\|^{2}+\frac{2\eta}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\|\mathbf{v}_{t}\|^{2}

∎

D.3
Proof of Lemma
11

Note that by definition of
E_{t}

\displaystyle\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{t})\|\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{t}\right\}\leq LD_{t}\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{t}\right\}\leq 2LD_{1}

We recall that
\mathbf{b}_{t}=\mathbb{E}[\mathbf{g}_{t}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}]-\mathbb{E}[\mathsf{clip}_{\Gamma}(\mathbf{g}_{t})|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}]
. Since
\mathsf{Cov}[\mathbf{g}_{t}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}]\preceq\Sigma
by Assumption
Bdd.

2
nd

Moment
, we obtain the following bound on
\|\mathbf{b}_{t}\|
by an application of Lemma
4

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{b}_{t}\|\leq\frac{\|\Sigma\|_{2}\sqrt{d_{\mathsf{eff}}}}{\Gamma}+\frac{\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{t})\|\sqrt{\|\Sigma\|_{2}}}{\Gamma}+\frac{\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{t})\|^{3}}{\Gamma^{2}}+\frac{\|\Sigma\|_{2}d_{\mathsf{eff}}\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{t})\|}{\Gamma^{2}}

Since
\tilde{\mathbf{b}}_{t}=\mathbf{b}_{t}\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{t}\right\}
, it follows that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{b}_{t}\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\|\Sigma\|_{2}\sqrt{d_{\mathsf{eff}}}}{\Gamma}+\frac{\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{t})\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{t}\right\}\|\sqrt{\|\Sigma\|_{2}}}{\Gamma}+\frac{\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{t})\|^{3}\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{t}\right\}}{\Gamma^{2}}+\frac{\|\Sigma\|_{2}d_{\mathsf{eff}}\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{t})\|\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{t}\right\}}{\Gamma^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\|\Sigma\|_{2}\sqrt{d_{\mathsf{eff}}}}{\Gamma}+\frac{2LD_{1}\sqrt{\|\Sigma\|_{2}}}{\Gamma}+\frac{8L^{3}D^{3}_{1}}{\Gamma^{2}}+\frac{2\|\Sigma\|_{2}d_{\mathsf{eff}}LD_{1}}{\Gamma^{2}}

D.4
Proof of Lemma
12

For any
s\in[T]
, we recall that
\mathbf{v}_{s}=\mathbb{E}\left[\mathsf{clip}_{\Gamma}(\mathbf{g}_{s})|\mathcal{F}_{s-1}\right]-\mathsf{clip}_{\Gamma}(\mathbf{g}_{s})
. Since
\mathbb{E}[\mathbf{g}_{s}|\mathcal{F}_{s-1}]=\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{s})
and
\mathsf{Cov}[\mathbf{g}_{s}|\mathcal{F}_{s-1}]\preceq\Sigma
, we obtain the following from Lemma
4

\displaystyle\|\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{v}_{s}\mathbf{v}_{s}^{T}|\mathcal{F}_{s-1}\right]\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\|\mathsf{Cov}\left[\mathsf{clip}_{\Gamma}(\mathbf{g}_{s})|\mathcal{F}_{s-1}\right]\|\leq\|\Sigma\|_{2}+\frac{\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{s})\|^{4}}{\Gamma^{2}}+\frac{\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{s})\|^{2}\mathsf{Tr}(\Sigma)}{\Gamma^{2}}

\displaystyle\mathsf{Tr}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{v}_{s}\mathbf{v}_{s}^{T}|\mathcal{F}_{s-1}\right]\right)
