\displaystyle=\mathsf{Tr}\left(\mathsf{Cov}\left[\mathsf{clip}_{\Gamma}(\mathbf{g}_{s})|\mathcal{F}_{s-1}\right]\right)\leq\mathsf{Tr}(\Sigma)

For
s\in[1:T]
define
\mathbb{E}[\tilde{\mathbf{v}}_{s}\tilde{\mathbf{v}}^{T}_{s}|\mathcal{F}_{s-1}]=\tilde{\Sigma}_{s}
. Since
\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{s}\right\}
is
\mathcal{F}_{s-1}
-measurable and
\tilde{\mathbf{v}}_{s}=\mathbf{v}_{s}\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{s}\right\}
, it follows that
\tilde{\Sigma}_{s}=\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{v}_{s}\mathbf{v}_{s}^{T}|\mathcal{F}_{s-1}\right]\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{s}\right\}
. Hence, we conclude the following from the above inequality

\displaystyle\|\tilde{\Sigma}_{s}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|\Sigma\|_{2}+\frac{\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{s})\|^{4}\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{s}\right\}}{\Gamma^{2}}+\frac{\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{s})\|^{2}\mathsf{Tr}(\Sigma)\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{s}\right\}}{\Gamma^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\|\Sigma\|_{2}+\frac{16L^{4}D^{4}_{1}}{\Gamma^{2}}+\frac{4L^{2}D^{2}_{1}\mathsf{Tr}(\Sigma)}{\Gamma^{2}}=V

\displaystyle\mathsf{Tr}(\tilde{\Sigma}_{s})

\displaystyle\leq\mathsf{Tr}(\Sigma)

(28)

For
s\in[T]
, define
h_{s}=\left\langle\tilde{\mathbf{v}}_{s},\mathbf{d}_{s}\right\rangle
. We note that

\displaystyle|h_{s}|

\displaystyle\leq\|\tilde{\mathbf{v}}_{s}\|\cdot\|\mathbf{d}_{s}\|\leq 4\Gamma D_{1}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[h_{s}|\mathcal{F}_{s-1}\right]

\displaystyle=\left\langle\mathbb{E}[\tilde{\mathbf{v}}_{s}|\mathcal{F}_{s-1}],\mathbf{d}_{s}\right\rangle=0

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[h^{2}_{s}|\mathcal{F}_{s-1}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbf{d}_{s}^{T}\mathbb{E}[\tilde{\mathbf{v}}_{s}\tilde{\mathbf{v}}^{T}_{s}]\mathbf{d}^{s}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{d}_{s}^{T}\tilde{\Sigma}_{s}\mathbf{d}_{s}

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{d}_{s}\|^{2}\|\tilde{\Sigma}\|\leq 4D^{2}_{1}V

Hence, by Freedman’s inequality (Lemma
3
), we conclude that the following holds with probability at least
1-\nicefrac{{\delta}}{{2}}
:

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left\langle\tilde{\mathbf{v}}_{t},\mathbf{d}_{t}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq 4D_{1}\sqrt{Vt\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}+8\Gamma D_{1}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})\quad\forall\ t\in[T]

We now apply Corollary
6
with
p_{s}=V
,
q_{s}=\mathsf{Tr}(\Sigma)
and
\tau=2\Gamma
to conclude that the following holds with probability at least
1-\nicefrac{{\delta}}{{2}}
uniformly for every
t\in[T]

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}\|\tilde{\mathbf{v}}_{s}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 4C_{M}\Gamma^{2}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{2}+C_{M}\mathsf{UP}(t)\mathsf{Tr}(\Sigma)+\frac{C_{M}t\mathsf{UP}(t)V^{2}}{4\Gamma^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq 4C_{M}\Gamma^{2}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{2}+C_{M}T\mathsf{Tr}(\Sigma)+\frac{C_{M}T^{2}V^{2}}{4\Gamma^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq C_{M}T\left(\|\Sigma\|_{2}d_{\mathsf{eff}}+\frac{4\Gamma^{2}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{2}}{T}+\frac{V^{2}T}{4\Gamma^{2}}\right)=C_{M}g^{2}T

where

\displaystyle g^{2}=\|\Sigma\|_{2}d_{\mathsf{eff}}+\frac{4\Gamma^{2}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{2}}{T}+\frac{V^{2}T}{4\Gamma^{2}}

The proof is concluded by a union bound

D.5
Proof of Lemma
13

We prove the claim via induction. Clearly, the claim is true for
t=1
. Now, suppose the claim holds for every
s\leq t
for some
t\in[T]
.
Since
\Pi_{\mathcal{C}}
is a contractive operator

\displaystyle D^{2}_{t+1}

\displaystyle=\|\mathbf{x}_{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{*}\|^{2}\leq D^{2}_{t}-2\eta\left\langle\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{t})-\mathbf{b}_{t}-\mathbf{v}_{t},\mathbf{x}_{t}-\mathbf{x}^{*}\right\rangle+\eta^{2}\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{t})-\mathbf{b}_{t}-\mathbf{v}_{t}\|

\displaystyle\leq D^{2}_{t}-2\eta\left\langle\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{t}),\mathbf{x}_{t}-\mathbf{x}^{*}\right\rangle+2\eta\left\langle\mathbf{b}_{t},\mathbf{x}_{t}-\mathbf{x}^{*}\right\rangle+2\eta\left\langle\mathbf{v}_{t},\mathbf{x}_{t}-\mathbf{x}^{*}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+2\eta^{2}\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{t})\|+4\eta^{2}\|\mathbf{v}_{t}\|^{2}+4\eta^{2}\|\mathbf{b}_{t}\|^{2}

By the coercivity property,

\displaystyle-2\eta\left\langle\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{t}),\mathbf{x}_{t}-\mathbf{x}^{*}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq-2\eta[F(\mathbf{x}_{t})-F(\mathbf{x}^{*})]-\frac{\eta}{L}\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{t})\|^{2}

Substituting this into the recurrence for
D^{2}_{t+1}
, we obtain the following:

\displaystyle D^{2}_{t+1}

\displaystyle\leq D^{2}_{t}-2\eta[F(\mathbf{x}_{t})-F(\mathbf{x}^{*})]+2\eta\left\langle\mathbf{v}_{t},\mathbf{x}_{t}-\mathbf{x}^{*}\right\rangle+2\eta\left\langle\mathbf{b}_{t},\mathbf{x}_{t}-\mathbf{x}^{*}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\eta(2\eta-\nicefrac{{1}}{{L}})\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{t})\|^{2}+4\eta^{2}\|\mathbf{v}_{t}\|^{2}+4\eta^{2}\|\mathbf{b}_{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq D^{2}_{t}+2\eta\left\langle\mathbf{v}_{t},\mathbf{x}_{t}-\mathbf{x}^{*}\right\rangle+2\eta\left\langle\mathbf{b}_{t},\mathbf{x}_{t}-\mathbf{x}^{*}\right\rangle+4\eta^{2}\|\mathbf{v}_{t}\|^{2}+4\eta^{2}\|\mathbf{b}_{t}\|^{2}

where we use the fact that
\eta\leq\nicefrac{{1}}{{2L}}
. Now, by the Cauchy Schwarz inequality and the fact that
ab\leq a^{2}+\nicefrac{{b^{2}}}{{4}}
we obtain the following:

\displaystyle 2\eta\left\langle\mathbf{b}_{t},\mathbf{x}_{t}-\mathbf{x}^{*}\right\rangle\leq\frac{D^{2}_{t}}{2T}+\eta^{2}T\|\mathbf{b}_{t}\|^{2}

It follows that

\displaystyle D^{2}_{t+1}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1+\frac{1}{2T}\right)D^{2}_{t}+5\eta^{2}T\|\mathbf{b}_{t}\|^{2}+4\eta^{2}\|\mathbf{v}_{t}\|^{2}-2\eta\left\langle\mathbf{v}_{t},\mathbf{x}_{t}-\mathbf{x}^{*}\right\rangle

Unrolling the above recursion for
t
steps and using the fact that
(1+\nicefrac{{1}}{{2T}})^{T}\leq 2
, we obtain the following:

\displaystyle D^{2}_{t+1}

\displaystyle\leq\left(1+\frac{1}{2T}\right)^{T}D^{2}_{1}+\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(1+\frac{1}{2T}\right)^{t-s}\left(5\eta^{2}T\|\mathbf{b}_{s}\|^{2}+4\eta^{2}\|\mathbf{v}_{s}\|^{2}+2\eta\left\langle\mathbf{v}_{s},\mathbf{x}_{s}-\mathbf{x}^{*}\right\rangle\right)

\displaystyle\leq 2D^{2}_{1}+\sum_{s=1}^{t}10\eta^{2}T\|\mathbf{b}_{s}\|^{2}+8\eta^{2}\|\mathbf{v}_{s}\|^{2}-4\eta\left\langle\mathbf{v}_{s},\mathbf{x}_{s}-\mathbf{x}^{*}\right\rangle

By the induction hypothesis,
\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{s}\right\}=1\ \forall s\in[t]
. Hence,

\displaystyle D^{2}_{t+1}

\displaystyle\leq 2D^{2}_{1}+10\eta^{2}T\sum_{s=1}^{t}\|\tilde{\mathbf{b}}_{s}\|^{2}+8\eta^{2}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\|\tilde{\mathbf{v}}_{s}\|^{2}-4\eta\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left\langle\tilde{\mathbf{v}}_{s},\mathbf{d}_{s}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq 2D^{2}_{1}+10\eta^{2}T^{2}B^{2}+8C_{M}\eta^{2}g^{2}T+16\eta D_{1}\left[\sqrt{Vt\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}+2\Gamma\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})\right]

\displaystyle\leq 3D^{2}_{1}+10\eta^{2}T^{2}B^{2}+8C_{M}\eta^{2}g^{2}T+64\eta^{2}\left(\sqrt{Vt\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}+2\Gamma\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 3D^{2}_{1}+10\eta^{2}T^{2}B^{2}+8C_{M}\eta^{2}g^{2}T+128\eta^{2}VT\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})+1024\Gamma^{2}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{2}

where the second inequality follows from the Lemma
11
and the fact that we have conditioned on
E
. Note that by definition of
g^{2}
and the AM-GM inequality

\displaystyle g^{2}T

\displaystyle\geq 4\Gamma^{2}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{2}+\frac{V^{2}T^{2}}{4\Gamma^{2}}\geq\max\{4\Gamma^{2}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{2},2VT\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})\}

It follows that

\displaystyle D^{2}_{t+1}

\displaystyle\leq 3D^{2}_{1}+10\eta^{2}T^{2}B^{2}+8(C_{M}+40)\eta^{2}g^{2}T

\displaystyle\leq 3D^{2}_{1}+10c^{2}D^{2}_{1}+c^{2}(8C_{M}+320)D^{2}_{1}

\displaystyle\leq 4D^{2}_{1}

where the second inequality uses the definition of
\eta
and the fact that
B^{\prime}
and
g^{\prime}
upper bound
B
and
G
respectively by equations (
25
) and (
27
) and the last inequality sets
c=\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{8C_{M}+330}}
. Hence,
D_{t+1}\leq 2D_{1}
which proves the claim by induction.

Appendix E
Analysis for Lipschitz Convex Functions

Let
d_{\mathsf{eff}}=\tfrac{\mathsf{Tr}(\Sigma)}{\|\Sigma\|_{2}}
. Since
\Sigma
is positive semidefinite,
1\leq d_{\mathsf{eff}}\leq d
. Moreover, let
\mathsf{clip}_{\Gamma}(\mathbf{g}_{t})=\partial F(\mathbf{x}_{t})+\mathbf{b}_{t}+\mathbf{v}_{t}
where
\mathbf{b}_{t}=\mathbb{E}[\mathsf{clip}_{\Gamma}(\mathbf{g}_{t})|\mathcal{F}_{t}]-\partial F(\mathbf{x}_{t})
represents the bias due to clipping and
\mathbb{E}[\mathbf{v}_{t}|\mathcal{F}_{t}]=0
. Let
D_{t}=\|\mathbf{x}_{t}-\mathbf{x}^{*}\|
where
\mathbf{x}^{*}
is the minimizer of
F
considered in the statement of Theorem
3
. Using the smoothness and convexity properties of
F
, we first prove the following intermediate average iterate guarantee:

Lemma 14

(Intermediate Average Iterate Guarantee)
.

The following holds for any
\eta>0

\displaystyle F(\hat{\mathbf{x}}_{T})-F(\mathbf{x}^{*})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{D^{2}_{1}}{2\eta T}-\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\langle\mathbf{b}_{t},\mathbf{x}_{t}-\mathbf{x}^{*}\right\rangle-\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\langle\mathbf{v}_{t},\mathbf{x}_{t}-\mathbf{x}^{*}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\eta G^{2}+\frac{2\eta}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\|\mathbf{b}_{t}\|^{2}+\frac{2\eta}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\|\mathbf{v}_{t}\|^{2}

Define the events
E_{t}
and the random vectors
\mathbf{d}_{t}
as follows for
t\in[T]
:

\displaystyle E_{t}

\displaystyle=\{D_{t}\leq 2D_{1}\}

\displaystyle\mathbf{d}_{t}

\displaystyle=(\mathbf{x}_{t}-\mathbf{x}^{*})\mathbbm{1}\left\{E_{t}\right\}

We use the following lemma to control the bias

Lemma 15

(Bias Control)
.

For every
t\in[T]
,
\|\mathbf{b}_{t}\|\leq B
where
B
is defined as follows:

\displaystyle B

\displaystyle=\frac{\|\Sigma\|_{2}\sqrt{d_{\mathsf{eff}}}}{\Gamma}+\frac{G\sqrt{\|\Sigma\|_{2}}}{\Gamma}+\frac{G^{3}}{\Gamma^{2}}+\frac{\|\Sigma\|_{2}d_{\mathsf{eff}}G}{\Gamma^{2}}

We use the following lemma to control the varince

Lemma 16

(Variance Control)
.

Let
V\geq 0
be defined as follows:

\displaystyle V

\displaystyle=\|\Sigma\|_{2}+\frac{G^{4}}{\Gamma^{2}}+\frac{G^{2}\|\Sigma\|_{2}d_{\mathsf{eff}}}{\Gamma^{2}}

Then the following holds with probability at least
1-\delta
uniformly for every
t\in[T]

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left\langle\mathbf{v}_{s},\mathbf{d}_{s}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq 4D_{1}\sqrt{Vt\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}+8\Gamma D_{1}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}\|\mathbf{v}_{s}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C_{M}g^{2}T

where
C_{M}
is a numerical constant and
g^{2}
is defined as follows

\displaystyle g^{2}

\displaystyle=\|\Sigma\|_{2}d_{\mathsf{eff}}+\frac{4\Gamma^{2}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{2}}{T}+\frac{V^{2}T}{4\Gamma^{2}}

Let
E
denote the following event

\displaystyle E=\{

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left\langle\mathbf{v}_{s},\mathbf{d}_{s}\right\rangle\leq 4D_{1}\sqrt{Vt\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}+8\Gamma D_{1}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})\quad\forall\ t\in[T]

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{t}\|\mathbf{v}_{s}\|^{2}\leq C_{M}g^{2}T\quad\forall t\in[T]\}

Note that by Lemma
16
,
\mathbb{P}(E)\geq 1-\delta
. We define the constant
A
as follows:

\displaystyle A=\|\Sigma\|_{2}\sqrt{d_{\mathsf{eff}}}+G\sqrt{\|\Sigma\|_{2}}=\sqrt{\|\Sigma\|_{2}}\left(\sqrt{\mathsf{Tr}(\Sigma)}+G\right)

We now set the clipping level
\Gamma=\sqrt{\tfrac{AT}{\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}}
. For this choice of
\Gamma
, we now simplify the expression for
B
as follows:

\displaystyle B=\sqrt{\frac{A\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}{T}}+\frac{G\left(\|\Sigma\|_{2}d_{\mathsf{eff}}+G^{2}\right)\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}{AT}

(29)

Similarly, the expression for
V
can be simplified as follows

\displaystyle V=\|\Sigma\|_{2}+\frac{G^{2}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}{AT}\left(\|\Sigma\|_{2}d_{\mathsf{eff}}+G^{2}\right)

(30)

Using the above inequality, we derive the following upper bound for
g
:

\displaystyle g

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\|\Sigma\|_{2}d_{\mathsf{eff}}}+\frac{2\Gamma\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}{\sqrt{T}}+\frac{V\sqrt{T}}{2\Gamma}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\|\Sigma\|_{2}d_{\mathsf{eff}}}+2\sqrt{A\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}+\frac{V\sqrt{\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}}{2\sqrt{A}}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\|\Sigma\|_{2}d_{\mathsf{eff}}}+2\sqrt{A\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}+\frac{\|\Sigma\|_{2}\sqrt{\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}}{2\sqrt{A}}+\frac{G^{2}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}}{A^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}T}\left(\|\Sigma\|_{2}d_{\mathsf{eff}}+G^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\|\Sigma\|_{2}d_{\mathsf{eff}}}+3\sqrt{A\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}+\frac{G^{2}\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}}{A^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}T}\left(\|\Sigma\|_{2}d_{\mathsf{eff}}+G^{2}\right)=g^{\prime}

(31)

We also prove the following uniform upper bound on the iterates
\mathbf{x}_{t}

Lemma 17

(Iterate Bound)
.

Let
\eta\leq c\min\{\nicefrac{{D_{1}}}{{BT}},\nicefrac{{D_{1}}}{{g^{\prime}\sqrt{T}}},\nicefrac{{D_{1}}}{{G\sqrt{T}}}\}
where
c=\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{8C_{M}+334}}
. Then, conditioned on the event
E
,
D_{t}\leq 2D_{1}\ \forall t\in[T]
.

Equipped with the above lemmas, we now prove the following theorem which is a formal restatement of Theorem
4

Theorem 8

(Lipschitz Convex Objectives)
.

Let Assumptions
Convexity
,

G

-Lipschitzness
and
Bdd.

2
nd

Moment
be satisfied. Then, for any
\delta\in(0,\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}})
and
T\geq\ln(\ln(d))
, there exists an
\eta\in(0,\nicefrac{{G}}{{\sqrt{T}}}]
such that the average iterate of Algorithm
1
run for
T
iterations with step-size
\eta_{t}=\eta
and clipping level
\Gamma=\sqrt{\tfrac{T\sqrt{\|\Sigma\|_{2}}(\sqrt{\mathsf{Tr}(\Sigma)}+G)}{\ln(\nicefrac{{\ln(T)}}{{\delta}})}}
satisfies the following with probability at least
1-\delta

\displaystyle F(\hat{\mathbf{x}}_{T})-F(\mathbf{x}^{*})

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{D_{1}G}{\sqrt{T}}+D_{1}\sqrt{\frac{\mathsf{Tr}(\Sigma)+\sqrt{\|\Sigma\|_{2}}\left(\sqrt{\mathsf{Tr}(\Sigma)}+G\right)\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}{T}}

\displaystyle+\frac{D_{1}G\ln(\nicefrac{{K}}{{\delta}})}{T}\sqrt{\frac{\mathsf{Tr}(\Sigma)+G^{2}}{\|\Sigma\|_{2}}}+\frac{D_{1}G^{2}\ln(\nicefrac{{1}}{{\delta}})^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}}{T^{\nicefrac{{3}}{{2}}}}\left(\frac{\mathsf{Tr}(\Sigma)+G^{2}}{\|\Sigma\|^{3}}\right)^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{4}}}

E.1
Proof of Lemma
14

Proof.

Since
\Pi_{\mathcal{C}}
is a contractive operator

\displaystyle D^{2}_{t+1}

\displaystyle=\|\mathbf{x}_{t+1}-\mathbf{x}^{*}\|^{2}\leq D^{2}_{t}-2\eta\left\langle\partial F(\mathbf{x}_{t})+\mathbf{b}_{t}+\mathbf{v}_{t},\mathbf{x}_{t}-\mathbf{x}^{*}\right\rangle+\eta^{2}\|\nabla F(\mathbf{x}_{t})+\mathbf{b}_{t}+\mathbf{v}_{t}\|

\displaystyle\leq D^{2}_{t}-2\eta\left\langle\partial F(\mathbf{x}_{t}),\mathbf{x}_{t}-\mathbf{x}^{*}\right\rangle-2\eta\left\langle\mathbf{b}_{t},\mathbf{x}_{t}-\mathbf{x}^{*}\right\rangle-2\eta\left\langle\mathbf{v}_{t},\mathbf{x}_{t}-\mathbf{x}^{*}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+2\eta^{2}\|\partial F(\mathbf{x}_{t})\|^{2}+4\eta^{2}\|\mathbf{v}_{t}\|^{2}+4\eta^{2}\|\mathbf{b}_{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq D^{2}_{t}-2\eta[F(\mathbf{x}_{t})-F(\mathbf{x}^{*})]-2\eta\left\langle\mathbf{b}_{t},\mathbf{x}_{t}-\mathbf{x}^{*}\right\rangle-2\eta\left\langle\mathbf{v}_{t},\mathbf{x}_{t}-\mathbf{x}^{*}\right\rangle+2\eta^{2}G^{2}+4\eta^{2}\|\mathbf{b}_{t}\|^{2}+4\eta^{2}\|\mathbf{v}_{t}\|^{2}

where the second inequality follows from the definition of the subgradient and the
G
lipschitzness of
F
. Rearranging and taking averages on both sides

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T}F(\mathbf{x}_{t})-F(\mathbf{x}^{*})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{D^{2}_{1}}{2\eta T}-\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\langle\mathbf{b}_{t},\mathbf{x}_{t}-\mathbf{x}^{*}\right\rangle-\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\langle\mathbf{v}_{t},\mathbf{x}_{t}-\mathbf{x}^{*}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\eta G^{2}+\frac{2\eta}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\|\mathbf{b}_{t}\|^{2}+\frac{2\eta}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\|\mathbf{v}_{t}\|^{2}

Using the above inequality and the convexity of
F
, we conclude that

\displaystyle F(\hat{\mathbf{x}}_{T})-F(\mathbf{x}^{*})

\displaystyle=F\left(\tfrac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbf{x}_{t}\right)-F(\mathbf{x}^{*})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}F(\mathbf{x}_{t})-F(\mathbf{x}^{*})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{D^{2}_{1}}{2\eta T}-\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\langle\mathbf{b}_{t},\mathbf{x}_{t}-\mathbf{x}^{*}\right\rangle-\frac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\langle\mathbf{v}_{t},\mathbf{x}_{t}-\mathbf{x}^{*}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+\eta G^{2}+\frac{2\eta}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\|\mathbf{b}_{t}\|^{2}+\frac{2\eta}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\|\mathbf{v}_{t}\|^{2}

∎

Proof of Lemma

\mathbf{b}_{t}=\mathbb{E}[\mathbf{g}_{t}|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}]-\mathbb{E}[\mathsf{clip}_{\Gamma}(\mathbf{g}_{t})|\mathcal{F}_{t-1}]-
. Since

nd
